<div class="container">
<h1>AI Assistant & Tool Call Flow</h1>
<p class="subtitle">Complete walkthrough of how the Generic Control Dashboard's AI Assistant processes requests, detects skills, executes tools, and streams responses.</p>

<h2>1. Architecture Overview</h2>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row" style="justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
  <div class="flow-box client"><strong>Frontend</strong><br><small>React SPA</small></div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box server"><strong>FastAPI</strong><br><small>/api/ai/chat</small></div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box ai"><strong>OpenAI Compatible</strong><br><small>OpenRouter API</small></div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row" style="justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 20px;">
  <div class="flow-box tool"><strong>Tool Executor</strong><br><small>ai_tools.py</small></div>
  <span class="arrow">←</span>
  <div class="flow-box server"><strong>Skills Loader</strong><br><small>skill_loader.py</small></div>
  <span class="arrow">←</span>
  <div class="flow-box ai"><strong>Model Response</strong><br><small>tool_calls</small></div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>2. Request Flow (Step by Step)</h2>
<div class="card">
<div class="code-block">
<pre>
POST /api/ai/chat
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "model": "minimax/minimax-m2.5:free",
  "messages": [
    {"role": "user", "content": "Read the config file and validate it"}
  ],
  "environment": "dev",
  "active_skills": []
}
</pre>
</div>
</div>

<h3>Step 1: Skill Detection</h3>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box client">User Message</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box server">detect_requested_skills()</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box ai">SKILL_ALIASES lookup</div>
</div>
</div>
<p>The system checks if any skills are triggered by keywords in the user message or explicitly requested via <code class="highlight">active_skills</code>.</p>

<h3>Step 2: Skill Planning Turn</h3>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box ai">For each detected skill → call planning LLM</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box server">planning_fn(user_message)</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box ai">Returns JSON skill plan</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="code-block">
<pre>
SKILL_ALIASES = {
    "tools": { planning_fn, system_fn, ... },
    "html_artifact": { planning_fn, system_fn, ... },
    "completeness_steps": { planning_fn, system_fn, instruction_fn, ... },
    "field_flow": { planning_fn, system_fn, ... },
    <span class="comment">// etc.</span>
}
</pre>
</div>

<h3>Step 3: System Prompt Assembly</h3>
<div class="code-block">
<pre>
provider_messages = [
    {"role": "system", "content": build_default_skill_system_prompt()},  <span class="comment">// Lists all skills</span>
    *skill_system_messages,  <span class="comment">// Per-skill system prompts from skill plans</span>
    *trimmed_messages       <span class="comment">// Conversation history (compacted if needed)</span>
]
</pre>
</div>

<h3>Step 4: Tool Eligibility Check</h3>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box server">_should_enable_tools()</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box" style="background:#1a3d1a; border-color:#7ee787;">
    <span class="badge badge-stream">ENABLED</span> if any true:
  </div>
</div>
<div style="margin-left:20px; margin-top:8px;">
  • "tools" in requested_skills<br>
  • "field_flow" in requested_skills<br>
  • "xlsx" in requested_skills<br>
  • Keywords like "read file", "execute command", "api call"<br>
  • Skill plan contains <code>should_use_tools: true</code>
</div>
</div>

<h2>3. Tool Call Execution Flow</h2>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row"><strong style="color:#8b949e; width:100%; margin-bottom:8px;">When tools are enabled (up to 3 turns):</strong></div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="step-number">1</div>
  <div class="flow-box ai">LLM responds with tool_calls=[...]</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="step-number">2</div>
  <div class="flow-box server">Parse each tool_call → emit SSE event: tool_call</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="step-number">3</div>
  <div class="flow-box tool">execute_tool(name, args) for ALL tools concurrently</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="step-number">4</div>
  <div class="flow-box server">Collect results → append as messages with role="tool"</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="step-number">5</div>
  <div class="flow-box ai">LLM processes results → final response</div>
</div>
</div>

<h3>execute_tool() Implementation</h3>
<div class="code-block">
<pre>
<span class="keyword">async def</span> <span class="function">execute_tool</span>(tool_name: str, tool_args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    <span class="keyword">if</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"read"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_read(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"write"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_write(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"read_excel"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_read_excel(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"write_excel"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_write_excel(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"shell"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_shell(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"glob"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_glob(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"grep"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_grep(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">elif</span> tool_name == <span class="string">"api_call"</span>:
        result = <span class="keyword">await</span> _execute_api_call(tool_args)
    <span class="keyword">else</span>:
        result = <span class="string">f"Unknown tool: {tool_name}"</span>

    <span class="comment"># Truncate to MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS (default 12000)</span>
    <span class="keyword">return</span> result[:MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS] + <span class="string">"... [truncated]"</span> <span class="keyword">if</span> len(result) > MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS <span class="keyword">else</span> result
</pre>
</div>

<h2>4. Available Tools</h2>
<table>
<tr><th>Tool</th><th>Parameters</th><th>Description</th></tr>
<tr><td class="code">read</td><td>filePath, limit?, offset?</td><td>Read file contents with optional line limiting</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">write</td><td>filePath, content</td><td>Write content to a file (creates parent dirs)</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">read_excel</td><td>filePath, sheet?, max_rows?</td><td>Read Excel (.xlsx/.xls) as tab-separated values</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">write_excel</td><td>filePath, data, sheet_name?</td><td>Write data to Excel (markdown table or JSON)</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">shell</td><td>command, workdir?</td><td>Execute shell command (subprocess, no shell injection)</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">glob</td><td>pattern, path?</td><td>Find files matching glob pattern recursively</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">grep</td><td>pattern, path?, include?</td><td>Search files by regex pattern</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api_call</td><td>endpoint, method?, body?, params?</td><td>Call internal backend API (relative paths only)</td></tr>
</table>

<h2>5. Security Controls</h2>
<div class="two-col">
<div class="card">
<h4>Path Access Control</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
AI_ALLOWED_PATHS=D:\data;C:\reports
<span class="comment"># Semicolon-separated; AI can only access these dirs</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="card">
<h4>Shell Command Control</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
AI_SHELL_ENABLED=false
AI_SHELL_ALLOWED_COMMANDS=git,python,pip
<span class="comment"># Blocked: pipes to shell, $() substitution, rm -rf</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="card">
<h4>API Endpoint Control</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
AI_API_ALLOWED_PREFIXES=/api/validator;/api/control
<span class="comment"># Only relative paths; external URLs blocked</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>6. Skills System</h2>
<p>Skills are loaded from two sources:</p>
<div class="two-col">
<div class="card">
<h4>TOML Skills</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
api/routers/ai_skills/
├── tools.toml
├── html_artifact.toml
├── dashboard_artifact.toml
├── form_artifact.toml
├── flowchart.toml
├── tableify.toml
├── graphify.toml
├── field_flow.toml
├── completeness_steps.toml
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="card">
<h4>Markdown Skills (SKILL.md)</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
api/routers/ai_skills/
├── transform_formula/SKILL.md
├── config_tester/SKILL.md
</pre></div>
</div>
</div>

<h3>Skill Configuration Structure</h3>
<div class="code-block">
<pre>
<span class="comment"># tools.toml example</span>
name = <span class="string">"tools"</span>
description = <span class="string">"Use read, write, read_excel, shell, grep, glob, api_call tools."</span>
triggers = [
    <span class="string">"read file"</span>, <span class="string">"write file"</span>, <span class="string">"execute command"</span>,
    <span class="string">"find files"</span>, <span class="string">"grep"</span>, <span class="string">"call api"</span>,
]

[planning_prompt]
template = <span class="string">"""You are the tools skill planner...{user_message}"""</span>

[system_prompt]
template = <span class="string">"""You have access to tools...{skill_plan}"""</span>
</pre>
</div>

<h2>7. SSE Response Format</h2>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div style="font-family: monospace; font-size: 0.85rem;">
<div style="margin: 4px 0;"><span style="color:#7ee787;">data:</span> {"type": "tool_call", "tool": "read_excel", "tool_label": "Reading Excel: config.xlsx"}</div>
<div style="margin: 4px 0;"><span style="color:#7ee787;">data:</span> {"content": "Chunk of AI response..."}</div>
<div style="margin: 4px 0;"><span style="color:#7ee787;">data:</span> {"done": true, "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1000, "completion_tokens": 200, "total_tokens": 1200}, "compacted": false}</div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>8. Control Execution Integration</h2>
<div class="flow-diagram">
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box client">User: "run completeness control"</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box server">completeness_steps skill activates</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box ai">LLM plans steps via instruction_fn()</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box server">Returns node instructions JSON</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box server">User can call control_runs API</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box tool">api_call tool → POST /api/control-runs/start</div>
</div>
<div class="flow-row">
  <div class="flow-box db">generic_controller.py subprocess spawned</div>
  <span class="arrow">→</span>
  <div class="flow-box db">task_storage/{task_id}.json</div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>9. Message Flow Sequence</h2>
<table class="sequence-table">
<tr><th></th><th>Component</th><th>Action</th></tr>
<tr><td>1</td><td>Frontend</td><td>POST /api/ai/chat with user message</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td>ai_assistant.py</td><td>detect_requested_skills() - check triggers</td></tr>
<tr><td>3</td><td>LLM (Planning)</td><td>For each skill: planning_fn() → skill plan JSON</td></tr>
<tr><td>4</td><td>ai_assistant.py</td><td>build_skill_system_messages() - compile system prompts</td></tr>
<tr><td>5</td><td>ai_assistant.py</td><td>_compact_messages() if context exceeds threshold</td></tr>
<tr><td>6</td><td>ai_assistant.py</td><td>_should_enable_tools() - determine if tool calling needed</td></tr>
<tr><td>7a</td><td>(if tools)</td><td>run_tool_enabled_chat() - multi-turn tool execution</td></tr>
<tr><td>7b</td><td>(if no tools)</td><td>Single completion from LLM</td></tr>
<tr><td>8</td><td>ai_assistant.py</td><td>emit_sse_chunks() - stream response in 50-char chunks</td></tr>
<tr><td>9</td><td>(optional)</td><td>asyncio.create_task(_silent_judge_and_save()) - sample judgment</td></tr>
</table>

<h2>10. Example: Complete Tool Call Roundtrip</h2>
<div class="card">
<h4>User Request</h4>
<div class="code-block"><pre>
<span class="string">"Read the Excel config at D:\data\config.xlsx and validate it using the API"</span>
</pre></div>
</div>

<div class="card" style="margin-top:16px;">
<h4>Flow</h4>
<div style="font-size: 0.9rem; line-height: 2;">
<div><span class="step-number">1</span> <strong>Skill Detection:</strong> triggers=["read excel", "api call"] → activates <span class="tag">tools</span></div>
<div><span class="step-number">2</span> <strong>Planning:</strong> returns {"should_use_tools": true, "tools": [{"name": "read_excel", ...}, {"name": "api_call", ...}]}</div>
<div><span class="step-number">3</span> <strong>System Prompt:</strong> includes tools.toml system prompt + tool definitions</div>
<div><span class="step-number">4</span> <strong>LLM Response:</strong> tool_calls=[{"read_excel", args}, {"api_call", args}]</div>
<div><span class="step-number">5</span> <strong>SSE Emit:</strong> data: {"tool_call": "read_excel", "tool_label": "Reading Excel: D:\data\config.xlsx"}</div>
<div><span class="step-number">6</span> <strong>Tool Exec:</strong> asyncio.gather(execute_tool("read_excel", ...), execute_tool("api_call", ...))</div>
<div><span class="step-number">7</span> <strong>Result Messages:</strong> role="tool", tool_call_id="xxx", content="Sheet: FieldOperations\nRows: 50\n..."</div>
<div><span class="step-number">8</span> <strong>Final LLM:</strong> Processes tool results → "The config file has 3 errors in FieldOperations sheet..."</div>
<div><span class="step-number">9</span> <strong>SSE Stream:</strong> Chunks of final response sent to client</div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>11. Key Files Reference</h2>
<table>
<tr><th>File</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/routers/ai_assistant.py</td><td>Main chat endpoint, skill detection, streaming</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/routers/ai_tools.py</td><td>Tool definitions (TOOL_DEFINITIONS) and execute_tool()</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/routers/ai_skills/skill_loader.py</td><td>Loads skills from .toml and SKILL.md files</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/control_execution/control_runner.py</td><td>Orchestrates control task execution</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/generic_controller.py</td><td>Subprocess that runs actual control logic</td></tr>
<tr><td class="code">api/routers/control_runs.py</td><td>REST API for starting/monitoring control runs</td></tr>
</table>

</div>
</body>

AI-ASSISTANT.p
"""
AI Assistant Router - Streaming chat endpoint for AI models
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from openai import AsyncOpenAI
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
import logging
import json
import os
import asyncio
import random
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from .ai_tools import TOOL_DEFINITIONS, execute_tool
from .ai_skills.skill_loader import SKILL_ALIASES, get_skill_builder

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/ai", tags=["AI Assistant"])

JUDGMENT_ENABLED = os.environ.get("JUDGMENT_ENABLED", "false").lower() == "true"
JUDGMENT_MODEL = os.environ.get("JUDGMENT_MODEL", "minimax/minimax-m2.5:free")
JUDGMENT_SAMPLE_RATE = float(os.environ.get("JUDGMENT_SAMPLE_RATE", "0.2"))
DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE = os.environ.get("DEFAULT_CRITERIA", "technical_accuracy")
SSE_CHUNK_SIZE = 50
# Keep the last N user/assistant message pairs to stay within token limits.
MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES = int(os.environ.get("AI_MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES", "20"))

# ── Context compaction ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Assumed context window for the configured model.  Override per deployment.
CONTEXT_MAX_TOKENS   = int(os.environ.get("AI_CONTEXT_MAX_TOKENS", "8192"))
# When estimated prompt tokens exceed this fraction of the window, compact.
COMPACTION_THRESHOLD = float(os.environ.get("AI_COMPACTION_THRESHOLD", "0.70"))
# After compaction, target this fraction of the window for old-message summary.
COMPACTION_TARGET    = float(os.environ.get("AI_COMPACTION_TARGET", "0.12"))
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CRITERIA_TEMPLATES = {
    "technical_accuracy": {
        "name": "Technical Accuracy",
        "criteria": [
            "Answer must be technically correct and factual",
            "Code examples must be syntactically valid and follow best practices",
            "No outdated or deprecated practices mentioned",
            "Information aligns with current industry standards"
        ]
    },
    "conciseness": {
        "name": "Conciseness",
        "criteria": [
            "Answer should be brief and to the point",
            "Avoid unnecessary verbosity or repetition",
            "Maximum 200 words unless complexity requires more",
            "Every sentence adds value"
        ]
    },
    "completeness": {
        "name": "Completeness",
        "criteria": [
            "Fully addresses all parts of the user's question",
            "Includes necessary context and explanations",
            "Provides actionable steps or examples where appropriate",
            "No important information is missing"
        ]
    },
    "code_quality": {
        "name": "Code Quality",
        "criteria": [
            "Code follows language-specific best practices and conventions",
            "Includes error handling where appropriate",
            "Code is readable, well-structured, and maintainable",
            "Variable and function names are descriptive"
        ]
    }
}


class Message(BaseModel):
    role: str
    content: str


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    model: str = Field(default="minimax/minimax-m2.5:free", description="Model name to use")
    temperature: float = Field(default=0.7, ge=0.0, le=2.0, description="Temperature for response randomness")
    messages: List[Message] = Field(..., description="Conversation messages")
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = Field(default=4096, description="Maximum tokens in response")
    environment: str = Field(default="dev", description="Environment (dev, poc, prod)")
    active_skills: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Optional assistant skills to activate")


def detect_requested_skills(request: ChatRequest, user_message: str) -> List[str]:
    requested = {skill.lower() for skill in request.active_skills}
    normalized_message = user_message.lower()

    for skill_name, skill_config in SKILL_ALIASES.items():
        if skill_name in requested:
            requested.add(skill_name)
            continue
        if any(trigger in normalized_message for trigger in skill_config["triggers"]):
            requested.add(skill_name)

    return [skill for skill in requested if skill in SKILL_ALIASES]


def build_default_skill_system_prompt() -> str:
    skill_list = ", ".join(SKILL_ALIASES.keys())
    return f"""You have {len(SKILL_ALIASES)} always-available response skills: {skill_list}.

Use them automatically when the user's request benefits from that format.

Output contracts:
- chart-json blocks must start with ```chart-json and contain valid JSON with type, title, xKey, yKeys, and data.
- flow-json blocks must start with ```flow-json and contain valid JSON with title, nodes, and edges. Nodes may include a details array.
- html-artifact blocks must start with ```html-artifact and contain one complete self-contained HTML document with inline CSS/JS only.
- markdown tables must not be wrapped in code blocks.
- completeness node instructions should name the node id, node label, objective, inputs, action/instruction, validation, and expected output.

Use only data that is present or safely inferable from the conversation. If needed data is missing, ask for it instead of inventing values."""


def _parse_json_object(content: str):
    if not content:
        return None

    cleaned = content.strip()
    if "```json" in cleaned:
        cleaned = cleaned.split("```json", 1)[1].split("```", 1)[0].strip()
    elif "```" in cleaned:
        cleaned = cleaned.split("```", 1)[1].split("```", 1)[0].strip()

    try:
        parsed = json.loads(cleaned)
        return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else None
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        start = cleaned.find("{")
        end = cleaned.rfind("}")
        if start != -1 and end != -1 and end > start:
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(cleaned[start:end + 1])
                return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else None
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return None
    return None


def _render_completeness_markdown(instruction_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    run_parameters = instruction_data.get("run_parameters", [])
    node_instructions = instruction_data.get("node_instructions", [])
    missing_information = instruction_data.get("missing_information", [])

    lines = ["## Run Parameters", ""]
    if run_parameters:
        lines.extend(["| Parameter | Required | Instruction |", "|---|---:|---|"])
        for param in run_parameters:
            lines.append(f"| {param.get('name', '')} | {param.get('required', True)} | {param.get('instruction', '')} |")
    else:
        lines.append("No run parameters were identified.")

    lines.extend(["", "## Node Instructions", ""])
    if node_instructions:
        lines.extend([
            "| Order | Node ID | Node | Inputs Needed | Instruction | Validation | Expected Output |",
            "|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|"
        ])
        for node in node_instructions:
            inputs = ", ".join(node.get("inputs_needed", []))
            validation = "<br>".join(node.get("validation", []))
            lines.append(
                f"| {node.get('order', '')} | {node.get('node_id', '')} | {node.get('node_name', '')} | "
                f"{inputs} | {node.get('instruction', '')} | {validation} | {node.get('expected_output', '')} |"
            )
    else:
        lines.append("No node instructions were generated.")

    if missing_information:
        lines.extend(["", "## Missing Information", ""])
        for item in missing_information:
            lines.append(f"- {item}")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _validate_skill_plan(skill_name: str, plan_data: Any) -> bool:
    """Validate that skill plan contains expected keys based on skill type."""
    if not isinstance(plan_data, dict):
        return False

    if SKILL_ALIASES.get(skill_name, {}).get("skill_format") == "markdown":
        return all(key in plan_data for key in ["should_use_markdown_skill", "skill_name"])

    required_keys = {
        "field_flow": ["should_use_field_flow"],
        "flowchart": ["should_flowchart", "title"],
        "tools": ["should_use_tools"],
        "html_artifact": ["should_create_html_artifact", "title"],
        "dashboard_artifact": ["should_create_dashboard_artifact", "title"],
        "form_artifact": ["should_create_form_artifact", "title"],
        "completeness_steps": ["goal", "required_parameters", "selected_nodes", "missing_information"],
    }

    required = required_keys.get(skill_name, [])
    return all(key in plan_data for key in required)


def _fallback_skill_plan(skill_name: str, user_message: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if skill_name == "html_artifact":
        return {
            "should_create_html_artifact": True,
            "artifact_type": "mini_app",
            "title": "HTML Artifact",
            "interaction_level": "interactive",
            "layout_model": "other",
            "data_needed": [],
            "visual_direction": "Professional, self-contained browser-native HTML artifact.",
            "acceptance_checks": ["Render in the assistant preview", "Use inline CSS and JavaScript only"],
            "fallback_plan": True,
        }

    if skill_name == "dashboard_artifact":
        return {
            "should_create_dashboard_artifact": True,
            "title": "Dashboard Artifact",
            "metrics": [],
            "sections": ["summary", "exceptions", "details"],
            "data_needed": [],
            "visual_direction": "Dense, readable internal operations dashboard.",
            "fallback_plan": True,
        }

    if skill_name == "form_artifact":
        return {
            "should_create_form_artifact": True,
            "title": "Interactive Form Artifact",
            "inputs": [],
            "outputs": [],
            "data_needed": [],
            "interaction_notes": "Client-side validation, calculated output, and reset behavior.",
            "fallback_plan": True,
        }

    if SKILL_ALIASES.get(skill_name, {}).get("skill_format") == "markdown":
        return {
            "should_use_markdown_skill": True,
            "skill_name": skill_name,
            "reason": "The user explicitly requested or triggered this skill.",
            "data_needed": [],
            "output_mode": "answer",
            "fallback_plan": True,
        }

    return None


async def _plan_single_skill(
    client: AsyncOpenAI,
    request: ChatRequest,
    skill_name: str,
    user_message: str,
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Plan one skill and return (skill_name, plan_json). Runs independently so callers can gather."""
    builder = get_skill_builder(skill_name)
    planning_fn = builder.get("planning")

    if planning_fn is None:
        return skill_name, "{}"

    try:
        if skill_name == "completeness_steps":
            plan_result = await client.chat.completions.create(
                model=request.model,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": planning_fn(user_message)}],
                temperature=0.2,
                max_tokens=900,
            )
            plan_content = extract_message_content(plan_result.choices[0].message.content)
            logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' raw planning response: {repr(plan_content[:500])}")
            completeness_plan = _parse_json_object(plan_content) or {
                "goal": user_message,
                "required_parameters": ["expectedRunDate", "inputConfigFilePath", "srcFilePath", "tgtFilePath"],
                "selected_nodes": [],
                "missing_information": []
            }

            if not isinstance(completeness_plan, dict) or "goal" not in completeness_plan:
                logger.warning(f"Invalid completeness_plan for {skill_name}, using default")
                completeness_plan = {
                    "goal": user_message,
                    "required_parameters": [],
                    "selected_nodes": [],
                    "missing_information": ["Failed to parse planning response"]
                }

            instruction_fn = builder.get("instruction")
            if instruction_fn:
                instruction_result = await client.chat.completions.create(
                    model=request.model,
                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": instruction_fn(user_message, completeness_plan)}],
                    temperature=0.2,
                    max_tokens=2200,
                )
                instruction_content = extract_message_content(instruction_result.choices[0].message.content)
                instruction_data = _parse_json_object(instruction_content)

                if instruction_data:
                    return skill_name, json.dumps({
                        "plan": completeness_plan,
                        "instructions": instruction_data,
                        "markdown": _render_completeness_markdown(instruction_data)
                    }, indent=2)
                return skill_name, instruction_content
        else:
            planning_prompt = planning_fn(user_message)
            logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' planning prompt: {repr(planning_prompt[:300])}")
            result = await client.chat.completions.create(
                model=request.model,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": planning_prompt}],
                temperature=0.2,
                max_tokens=1500,
            )
            plan_content = extract_message_content(result.choices[0].message.content)
            logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' raw planning response: {repr(plan_content[:500])}")
            parsed_plan = _parse_json_object(plan_content)
            logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' parsed plan: {parsed_plan}")

            if parsed_plan is None or not _validate_skill_plan(skill_name, parsed_plan):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid or incomplete planning response for {skill_name}: {plan_content[:200] if plan_content else 'empty'}")
                fallback_plan = _fallback_skill_plan(skill_name, user_message)
                return skill_name, json.dumps(fallback_plan) if fallback_plan else "{}"
            if parsed_plan.get("should_use_markdown_skill") is False:
                logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' planner declined this request")
                return skill_name, "{}"
            return skill_name, json.dumps(parsed_plan)

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to run planning for skill '{skill_name}': {e}", exc_info=True)
        fallback_plan = _fallback_skill_plan(skill_name, user_message)
        return skill_name, json.dumps(fallback_plan) if fallback_plan else "{}"

    return skill_name, "{}"


async def run_skill_planning_turn(client: AsyncOpenAI, request: ChatRequest, user_message: str, skills: List[str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Run planning for all triggered skills concurrently."""
    skills_with_planners = [s for s in skills if get_skill_builder(s).get("planning") is not None]
    if not skills_with_planners:
        return {}

    results = await asyncio.gather(
        *[_plan_single_skill(client, request, s, user_message) for s in skills_with_planners],
        return_exceptions=True,
    )

    plans: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for item in results:
        if isinstance(item, Exception):
            logger.error(f"Skill planning task raised an exception: {item}", exc_info=item)
            continue
        skill_name, plan_content = item
        plans[skill_name] = plan_content

    return plans


def build_skill_system_messages(skill_plans: Dict[str, str]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    messages = []

    for skill_name, plan_content in skill_plans.items():
        logger.info(f"Building system message for skill '{skill_name}', plan_content: {repr(plan_content[:200] if plan_content else 'empty')}")
        if not plan_content or plan_content == "{}":
            logger.info(f"Skipping skill '{skill_name}' - empty plan")
            continue

        builder = get_skill_builder(skill_name)
        system_fn = builder.get("system")

        if system_fn is None:
            logger.info(f"Skipping skill '{skill_name}' - no system_fn")
            continue

        try:
            parsed_plan = json.loads(plan_content)
            logger.info(f"Calling system_fn for skill '{skill_name}' with parsed plan: {parsed_plan}")
            system_prompt = system_fn(plan_content)
            logger.info(f"Skill '{skill_name}' system prompt (first 200 chars): {repr(system_prompt[:200])}")
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_prompt})
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            logger.error(f"Invalid JSON in skill plan for '{skill_name}': {e}, content: {repr(plan_content[:200])}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to build system prompt for skill '{skill_name}': {e}")

    return messages


def get_openai_api_key() -> str:
    api_key = os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
    if api_key:
        return api_key
    raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="No API key configured. Set OPENAI_API_KEY.")


def get_api_base_url() -> str:
    custom_endpoint = os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_BASE_URL", "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1")
    if custom_endpoint:
        return custom_endpoint.rstrip("/")
    return "https://api.openai.com/v1"


_openai_client: Optional[AsyncOpenAI] = None


def get_openai_client() -> AsyncOpenAI:
    """Return the module-level AsyncOpenAI singleton, creating it on first call.

    Reusing one client across requests preserves the connection pool and avoids
    the overhead of TLS handshakes on every chat request.
    """
    global _openai_client
    if _openai_client is None:
        _openai_client = AsyncOpenAI(
            api_key=get_openai_api_key(),
            base_url=get_api_base_url(),
            timeout=120,
        )
    return _openai_client


def extract_message_content(message_content: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(message_content, str):
        return message_content

    if isinstance(message_content, list):
        text_parts = []
        for item in message_content:
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                if item.get("type") == "text" and item.get("text"):
                    text_parts.append(item["text"])
                elif item.get("type") == "output" and item.get("output"):
                    text_parts.append(item["output"])
            else:
                item_type = getattr(item, "type", None)
                item_text = getattr(item, "text", None) or getattr(item, "output", None)
                if item_type in ("text", "output") and item_text:
                    text_parts.append(item_text)
        return "".join(text_parts)

    if hasattr(message_content, "content"):
        content = message_content.content
        if isinstance(content, str):
            return content
        if isinstance(content, list):
            return extract_message_content(content)

    return ""


async def emit_sse_chunks(content: str):
    for i in range(0, len(content), SSE_CHUNK_SIZE):
        chunk_content = content[i:i + SSE_CHUNK_SIZE]
        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'content': chunk_content})}\n\n"
        await asyncio.sleep(0)


def _should_enable_tools(requested_skills: List[str], skill_plans: Dict[str, str], user_message: str) -> bool:
    if "tools" in requested_skills:
        return True

    # Some skills, like field_flow, need read_excel even when the user did not
    # explicitly say "use tools".
    if "field_flow" in requested_skills:
        return True

    if "xlsx" in requested_skills:
        return True

    tool_words = (
        "read file", "write file", "read excel", "read xlsx", "spreadsheet",
        "run command", "execute command", "shell", "grep", "glob",
        "find files", "search files", "call api", "api call",
    )
    normalized = user_message.lower()
    if any(word in normalized for word in tool_words):
        return True

    for plan_content in skill_plans.values():
        plan = _parse_json_object(plan_content)
        if not plan:
            continue
        if plan.get("should_use_tools") or plan.get("excel_file"):
            return True
        if plan.get("skill_name") == "xlsx":
            return True
        if plan.get("skill_name") == "web-artifacts-builder" and plan.get("output_mode") in ("tool_use", "file_artifact"):
            return True

    return False


def _tool_call_to_message(tool_call: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": tool_call.id,
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": tool_call.function.name,
            "arguments": tool_call.function.arguments or "{}",
        },
    }


def _parse_tool_arguments(raw_arguments: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not raw_arguments:
        return {}

    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw_arguments)
        return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        parsed = _parse_json_object(raw_arguments)
        return parsed or {}


def _tool_call_label(tool_name: str, tool_args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Return a human-readable status label for a tool call."""
    if tool_name == "read":
        return f"Reading file: {tool_args.get('filePath', '')}"
    if tool_name == "write":
        return f"Writing file: {tool_args.get('filePath', '')}"
    if tool_name == "read_excel":
        return f"Reading Excel: {tool_args.get('filePath', '')}"
    if tool_name == "write_excel":
        return f"Writing Excel: {tool_args.get('filePath', '')}"
    if tool_name == "shell":
        cmd = tool_args.get('command', '')
        return f"Running command: {cmd[:60]}{'…' if len(cmd) > 60 else ''}"
    if tool_name == "glob":
        return f"Searching files: {tool_args.get('pattern', '')}"
    if tool_name == "grep":
        return f"Searching for: {tool_args.get('pattern', '')}"
    if tool_name == "api_call":
        return f"Calling API: {tool_args.get('method', 'GET')} {tool_args.get('endpoint', '')}"
    return f"Running tool: {tool_name}"


async def run_tool_enabled_chat(
    client: AsyncOpenAI,
    request: ChatRequest,
    provider_messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
):
    """Async generator yielding tool_call status events and a final content event."""
    messages = [
        {
            "role": "system",
            "content": (
                "You may call tools when external file, Excel, shell, search, or API data is needed. "
                f"The backend working directory is {Path.cwd()}. Prefer absolute paths when a file path is supplied. "
                "After tools run, answer using the tool results rather than pretending the data was unavailable."
            ),
        },
        *provider_messages,
    ]

    usage_acc: Dict[str, int] = {"prompt_tokens": 0, "completion_tokens": 0, "total_tokens": 0}

    for turn in range(3):
        result = await client.chat.completions.create(
            model=request.model,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=request.temperature,
            max_tokens=request.max_tokens,
            tools=TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
            tool_choice="auto",
        )
        _accumulate_usage(usage_acc, result.usage)
        choice = result.choices[0]
        message = choice.message
        tool_calls = message.tool_calls or []

        logger.info(
            "Tool chat turn %s finish_reason=%s tool_calls=%s",
            turn + 1,
            choice.finish_reason,
            len(tool_calls),
        )

        if not tool_calls:
            yield {"type": "final", "content": extract_message_content(message.content), "usage": usage_acc}
            return

        messages.append({
            "role": "assistant",
            "content": message.content or "",
            "tool_calls": [_tool_call_to_message(tc) for tc in tool_calls],
        })

        # Parse args once; emit all labels immediately so the frontend shows
        # every pending tool before any of them starts executing.
        parsed_calls = [
            (tc, tc.function.name, _parse_tool_arguments(tc.function.arguments))
            for tc in tool_calls
        ]
        for tc, name, targs in parsed_calls:
            yield {"type": "tool_call", "tool": name, "label": _tool_call_label(name, targs)}

        # Execute all tools in this turn concurrently.
        async def _run_tool(tc, name, targs):
            logger.info("Executing tool '%s' with args: %s", name, targs)
            result = await execute_tool(name, targs)
            logger.info("Tool '%s' result preview: %s", name, result[:200])
            return tc.id, result

        tool_results = await asyncio.gather(
            *[_run_tool(tc, name, targs) for tc, name, targs in parsed_calls]
        )

        for call_id, tool_result in tool_results:
            messages.append({
                "role": "tool",
                "tool_call_id": call_id,
                "content": tool_result,
            })

    logger.warning("Tool chat reached max tool turns; asking model for final response")
    result = await client.chat.completions.create(
        model=request.model,
        messages=[
            *messages,
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": "Tool execution loop limit reached. Summarize the available tool results and clearly mention anything still missing.",
            },
        ],
        temperature=request.temperature,
        max_tokens=request.max_tokens,
    )
    _accumulate_usage(usage_acc, result.usage)
    yield {"type": "final", "content": extract_message_content(result.choices[0].message.content), "usage": usage_acc}


async def judge_response(
    user_message: str,
    ai_response: str,
    criteria_template: str = DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE,
    model: str = JUDGMENT_MODEL
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not JUDGMENT_ENABLED:
        return None

    if criteria_template not in CRITERIA_TEMPLATES:
        criteria_template = DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE

    criteria = CRITERIA_TEMPLATES[criteria_template]
    criteria_list = "\n".join([f"- {c}" for c in criteria["criteria"]])

    judge_prompt = f"""You are an expert evaluator. Judge the following AI response based on these criteria:

{criteria_list}

User's Question:
{user_message}

AI Response to Judge:
{ai_response}

Provide your evaluation in JSON format:
{{
  "score": <0-100>,
  "reasoning": "<brief explanation>",
  "passed": <true if score >= 60, false otherwise>
}}

Be objective and fair. Consider the question's complexity when evaluating."""

    try:
        start_time = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
        client = get_openai_client()
        result = await client.chat.completions.create(
            model=model,
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": judge_prompt}],
            temperature=0.3,
            max_tokens=500,
        )
        content = extract_message_content(result.choices[0].message.content)

        if "```json" in content:
            content = content.split("```json")[1].split("```")[0].strip()
        elif "```" in content:
            content = content.split("```")[1].split("```")[0].strip()

        judgment = json.loads(content.strip())

        duration_ms = int((asyncio.get_running_loop().time() - start_time) * 1000)
        judgment["judge_model"] = model
        judgment["judgment_duration_ms"] = duration_ms

        logger.info(f"Judged response: score={judgment.get('score')}, passed={judgment.get('passed')}")
        return judgment

    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to parse judgment JSON: {str(e)}")
        return None
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Judgment error: {str(e)}")
        return None


def _estimate_tokens(messages: List[Message]) -> int:
    """Rough token estimate: 1 token ≈ 4 characters (good enough for threshold checks)."""
    return sum(len(m.content) for m in messages) // 4


async def _compact_messages(
    client: AsyncOpenAI,
    model: str,
    messages: List[Message],
) -> tuple[List[Message], bool]:
    """Summarise older messages when estimated tokens exceed COMPACTION_THRESHOLD.

    Returns (messages, was_compacted).  The oldest messages are replaced with a
    single system-role summary; recent messages are kept verbatim so the model
    retains immediate context.
    """
    estimated = _estimate_tokens(messages)
    threshold = int(CONTEXT_MAX_TOKENS * COMPACTION_THRESHOLD)

    if estimated <= threshold:
        return messages, False

    # Work out how many recent messages fit inside the target budget.
    target_chars = int(CONTEXT_MAX_TOKENS * COMPACTION_TARGET) * 4
    recent: List[Message] = []
    chars_kept = 0
    for msg in reversed(messages):
        msg_len = len(msg.content)
        if chars_kept + msg_len <= target_chars:
            recent.insert(0, msg)
            chars_kept += msg_len
        else:
            break

    to_summarise = messages[: len(messages) - len(recent)]
    if not to_summarise:
        return messages, False

    history_text = "\n\n".join(
        f"{m.role.upper()}: {m.content}" for m in to_summarise
    )

    logger.info(
        "Compacting %d messages (~%d estimated tokens, threshold %d). Keeping %d recent.",
        len(to_summarise), estimated, threshold, len(recent),
    )

    try:
        summary_result = await client.chat.completions.create(
            model=model,
            messages=[{
                "role": "user",
                "content": (
                    "Summarise the following conversation history. "
                    "Preserve key facts, file paths, errors, decisions, and any "
                    "context needed to continue the conversation. Be concise.\n\n"
                    + history_text
                ),
            }],
            temperature=0.1,
            max_tokens=600,
        )
        summary_text = extract_message_content(summary_result.choices[0].message.content)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Compaction summary call failed: %s — skipping compaction", e)
        return messages, False

    summary_msg = Message(
        role="system",
        content=f"[Earlier conversation compacted — summary]\n{summary_text}",
    )

    logger.info("Compaction complete. %d → 1 summary + %d recent messages.", len(to_summarise), len(recent))
    return [summary_msg] + recent, True


def _accumulate_usage(acc: Dict[str, int], usage) -> None:
    """Add provider usage counters into a running total dict."""
    if not usage:
        return
    acc["prompt_tokens"]     += getattr(usage, "prompt_tokens",     0) or 0
    acc["completion_tokens"] += getattr(usage, "completion_tokens", 0) or 0
    acc["total_tokens"]      += getattr(usage, "total_tokens",      0) or 0


def _trim_messages(messages: List[Message]) -> List[Message]:
    """Keep only the most recent MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES to avoid exceeding token limits."""
    if len(messages) <= MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES:
        return messages
    trimmed = len(messages) - MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES
    logger.info("Trimming %d old message(s) from history (keeping last %d)", trimmed, MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES)
    return messages[-MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES:]


async def stream_chat_response(request: ChatRequest):
    full_response = ""
    user_message = ""

    try:
        client = get_openai_client()

        for msg in reversed(request.messages):
            if msg.role == "user":
                user_message = msg.content
                break

        requested_skills = detect_requested_skills(request, user_message)

        logger.info(
            "AI Chat request: model=%s temp=%s messages=%d env=%s skills=%s",
            request.model, request.temperature, len(request.messages),
            request.environment, requested_skills,
        )

        skill_plans = await run_skill_planning_turn(client, request, user_message, requested_skills) if requested_skills else {}
        skill_system_messages = build_skill_system_messages(skill_plans)

        compacted_messages, was_compacted = await _compact_messages(client, request.model, request.messages)
        trimmed_messages = _trim_messages(compacted_messages)
        provider_messages = [
            {"role": "system", "content": build_default_skill_system_prompt()},
            *skill_system_messages,
            *[{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in trimmed_messages]
        ]

        usage: Dict[str, int] = {"prompt_tokens": 0, "completion_tokens": 0, "total_tokens": 0}

        if _should_enable_tools(requested_skills, skill_plans, user_message):
            async for event in run_tool_enabled_chat(client, request, provider_messages):
                if event["type"] == "tool_call":
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'tool_call': event['tool'], 'tool_label': event['label']})}\n\n"
                elif event["type"] == "final":
                    full_response = event["content"]
                    event_usage = event.get("usage") or {}
                    usage["prompt_tokens"]     = event_usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
                    usage["completion_tokens"] = event_usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
                    usage["total_tokens"]      = event_usage.get("total_tokens", 0)
        else:
            result = await client.chat.completions.create(
                model=request.model,
                messages=provider_messages,
                temperature=request.temperature,
                max_tokens=request.max_tokens,
            )
            full_response = extract_message_content(result.choices[0].message.content)
            _accumulate_usage(usage, result.usage)

        if not full_response:
            logger.warning("AI provider returned an empty response")
            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': 'Empty response from AI provider'})}\n\n"
            return

        async for chunk in emit_sse_chunks(full_response):
            yield chunk

        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'done': True, 'usage': usage, 'compacted': was_compacted})}\n\n"

        # Sample judgments — running on every response doubles API cost for no extra signal.
        if JUDGMENT_ENABLED and full_response and user_message and random.random() < JUDGMENT_SAMPLE_RATE:
            asyncio.create_task(
                _silent_judge_and_save(
                    user_message=user_message,
                    ai_response=full_response,
                    model=request.model,
                    temperature=request.temperature,
                    environment=request.environment,
                )
            )

    except HTTPException as e:
        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': e.detail})}\n\n"
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"AI Chat error: {str(e)}")
        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': str(e)})}\n\n"


async def _silent_judge_and_save(
    user_message: str,
    ai_response: str,
    model: str,
    temperature: float,
    environment: str = "dev",
):
    try:
        from .judgment_service import JudgmentService
        import time

        judgment = await judge_response(
            user_message=user_message,
            ai_response=ai_response,
            criteria_template=DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE,
            model=JUDGMENT_MODEL
        )

        if judgment:
            judgment_data = {
                "judgment_id": f"judgment-{int(time.time())}-{os.urandom(4).hex()}",
                "chat_id": "unknown",
                "message_id": "unknown",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat() + "Z",
                "model_used": model,
                "criteria_template": DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE,
                "criteria_list": CRITERIA_TEMPLATES[DEFAULT_CRITERIA_TEMPLATE]["criteria"],
                "user_message": user_message,
                "ai_response": ai_response,
                "judgment": judgment,
                "metadata": {
                    "temperature": temperature,
                    "response_length": len(ai_response),
                    "user_id": "anonymous",
                    "environment": environment,
                }
            }
            JudgmentService.save_judgment(judgment_data)
            logger.info(f"Silently judged and saved: score={judgment.get('score')}")

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Silent judgment failed: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/chat")
async def chat(request: ChatRequest):
    return StreamingResponse(
        stream_chat_response(request),
        media_type="text/event-stream",
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
            "Connection": "keep-alive",
            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no"
        }
    )

async def get_available_models():
    models = [
        {"id": "minimax/minimax-m2.5:free", "name": "Minimax", "description": "Fast and cost-effective"}
    ]
    return {"models": models}

        "skills": [
            {
                "id": skill_name,
                "name": config["name"],
                "description": config["description"],
                "triggers": list(config["triggers"]),
                "skill_format": config.get("skill_format", "toml"),
                "source_file": config.get("source_file")
            }
            for skill_name, config in SKILL_ALIASES.items()
        ]
    }

    return {"tools": TOOL_DEFINITIONS}


@router.get("/health")
async def health_check():
    try:
        has_openai_key = bool(os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"))
        return {
            "status": "healthy" if has_openai_key else "not_configured",
            "providers": {
                "openai": has_openai_key,
                "base_url": get_api_base_url()
            }
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}

"""
AI Assistant Tools - File, shell, and API tools for the AI assistant.
"""
import json
import logging
import re
import shlex
import subprocess
import fnmatch
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# Max characters returned from any single tool call before truncation.
# Keeps the model context window sane on large file/command outputs.
# Override with AI_MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS env var.
MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS = int(os.environ.get("AI_MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS", "12000"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Path access control
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Set AI_ALLOWED_PATHS in your environment as a semicolon-separated list of
# absolute directory paths that the AI is permitted to read/write.
#
#   AI_ALLOWED_PATHS=D:\office\rag_app\controldash\data;C:\shared\reports
#
# Leave unset (or empty) to allow all paths (not recommended for production).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _get_allowed_paths() -> List[Path]:
    raw = os.environ.get("AI_ALLOWED_PATHS", "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return []
    return [Path(p.strip()).resolve() for p in raw.split(";") if p.strip()]


def _is_path_allowed(target: Path) -> bool:
    allowed = _get_allowed_paths()
    if not allowed:
        return True  # no restriction configured
    resolved = target.resolve()
    return any(
        resolved == base or base in resolved.parents
        for base in allowed
    )


def _check_path(target: Path) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError if target is outside every allowed directory."""
    if not _is_path_allowed(target):
        allowed = _get_allowed_paths()
        allowed_str = ", ".join(str(p) for p in allowed)
        raise ValueError(
            f"Access denied: '{target}' is outside the allowed directories: {allowed_str}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# In-process app registry
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# When the FastAPI app registers itself here, api_call uses the ASGI transport
# (zero network, works in dev and prod regardless of bind address).
# Falls back to a real HTTP call if the app was never registered.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_fastapi_app = None


def register_app(app) -> None:
    global _fastapi_app
    _fastapi_app = app


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shell access control
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AI_SHELL_ENABLED=false  — disable the shell tool entirely (recommended).
# AI_SHELL_ALLOWED_COMMANDS=git,python,pip  — comma-separated command
#   prefixes that are permitted when shell is enabled.  Leave unset to allow
#   all commands (only do this in a fully trusted environment).
#
# Dangerous patterns (pipes to shells, command substitution, etc.) are always
# blocked regardless of the allowlist.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_SHELL_DANGEROUS_PATTERNS = re.compile(
    r"(\|\s*sh\b|\|\s*bash\b|\|\s*cmd\b|\|\s*powershell\b"
    r"|`[^`]+`|\$\([^)]+\)"          # backtick / $() substitution
    r"|>\s*/dev/|>\s*\\\\|rm\s+-rf"  # destructive redirects / rm -rf
    r"|format\s+[a-z]:|del\s+/[sq])" # Windows format/del /s
    , re.IGNORECASE
)


def _check_shell(command: str) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError if shell execution is disabled or the command is not allowed."""
    enabled = os.environ.get("AI_SHELL_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower()
    if enabled in ("false", "0", "no"):
        raise ValueError("Shell tool is disabled (AI_SHELL_ENABLED=false)")

    if _SHELL_DANGEROUS_PATTERNS.search(command):
        raise ValueError("Command blocked: contains a disallowed pattern")

    raw_allowed = os.environ.get("AI_SHELL_ALLOWED_COMMANDS", "").strip()
    if not raw_allowed:
        return  # no allowlist — all commands permitted

    # Parse with shlex so "C:\Python\python.exe" is the real first token,
    # not a naive split() which breaks on quoted paths.
    try:
        parsed = shlex.split(command)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError("Command blocked: could not be safely parsed")

    if not parsed:
        raise ValueError("Command blocked: empty command")

    # Strip path so "C:\Python\python.exe" matches allowlist entry "python"
    first_token_name = Path(parsed[0]).stem.lower()
    allowed_prefixes = [p.strip().lower() for p in raw_allowed.split(",") if p.strip()]
    if not any(first_token_name == p or first_token_name.startswith(p) for p in allowed_prefixes):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Command '{first_token_name}' is not in the allowed list: {raw_allowed}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# API call access control
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# api_call always targets the same backend (API_BASE_URL / localhost:8000).
# The AI cannot supply a full http:// URL — only relative endpoint paths.
#
# AI_API_ALLOWED_PREFIXES=/api/validator;/api/completeness;/api/control
#   Semicolon-separated endpoint prefixes the AI may call.
#   Leave unset to allow all endpoints on this backend.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _check_api_endpoint(endpoint: str) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError if the endpoint is an external URL or not in the allowlist."""
    # Block any attempt to call an external host
    if re.match(r"https?://", endpoint.strip(), re.IGNORECASE):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Access denied: external URLs are not allowed. "
            f"Provide a relative path such as '/api/validator/run'."
        )

    raw_allowed = os.environ.get("AI_API_ALLOWED_PREFIXES", "").strip()
    if not raw_allowed:
        return  # no restriction — all local endpoints allowed

    allowed_prefixes = [p.strip() for p in raw_allowed.split(";") if p.strip()]
    normalised = "/" + endpoint.lstrip("/")
    if not any(normalised.startswith(prefix) for prefix in allowed_prefixes):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Endpoint '{endpoint}' is not in the allowed list: {raw_allowed}"
        )

def parse_json_object(content: str):
    """Parse JSON from plain JSON or fenced JSON content."""
    parsed = parse_json_value(content)
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else None


def parse_json_value(content: str):
    """Parse JSON from plain JSON or fenced JSON content."""
    if not content:
        return None

    cleaned = content.strip()
    if "```json" in cleaned:
        cleaned = cleaned.split("```json", 1)[1].split("```", 1)[0].strip()
    elif "```" in cleaned:
        cleaned = cleaned.split("```", 1)[1].split("```", 1)[0].strip()

    try:
        return json.loads(cleaned)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        start = cleaned.find("{")
        end = cleaned.rfind("}") if start != -1 else -1
        if start == -1:
            start = cleaned.find("[")
            end = cleaned.rfind("]")
        if start != -1 and end != -1 and end > start:
            try:
                return json.loads(cleaned[start:end + 1])
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return None
    return None


async def execute_tool(tool_name: str, tool_args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Execute a tool and return its result, truncated to MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS."""
    try:
        if tool_name == "read":
            result = await _execute_read(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "write":
            result = await _execute_write(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "read_excel":
            result = await _execute_read_excel(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "write_excel":
            result = await _execute_write_excel(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "shell":
            result = await _execute_shell(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "glob":
            result = await _execute_glob(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "grep":
            result = await _execute_grep(tool_args)
        elif tool_name == "api_call":
            result = await _execute_api_call(tool_args)
        else:
            return f"Unknown tool: {tool_name}"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Tool execution error: {str(e)}"

    if len(result) > MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS:
        result = (
            result[:MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS]
            + f"\n\n[... truncated — {len(result):,} chars total, showing first {MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS:,}]"
        )
    return result


async def _execute_read(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    file_path = Path(args["filePath"])
    limit = int(args.get("limit", 2000))
    offset = int(args.get("offset", 1))

    try:
        _check_path(file_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if not file_path.exists():
        return f"Error: File not found: {file_path}"
    if not file_path.is_file():
        return f"Error: Path is not a file: {file_path}"

    content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = content.split("\n")
    start = max(0, offset - 1)
    end = start + limit
    return "\n".join(lines[start:end])


async def _execute_write(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    file_path = Path(args["filePath"])
    try:
        _check_path(file_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_path.write_text(args["content"], encoding="utf-8")
    return f"Successfully wrote to {file_path}"


async def _execute_read_excel(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    import openpyxl

    file_path = Path(args["filePath"])
    try:
        _check_path(file_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if not file_path.exists():
        return f"Error: File not found: {file_path}"

    sheet_name = args.get("sheet")
    max_rows = int(args.get("max_rows", 100))

    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(file_path, data_only=True)

    if sheet_name:
        sheet_name = str(sheet_name)
        if sheet_name.isdigit():
            sheet_index = int(sheet_name) - 1
            if sheet_index < 0 or sheet_index >= len(wb.worksheets):
                wb.close()
                return f"Error: Sheet index out of range: {sheet_name}. Available sheets: {', '.join(wb.sheetnames)}"
            ws = wb.worksheets[sheet_index]
        else:
            if sheet_name not in wb.sheetnames:
                wb.close()
                return f"Error: Sheet not found: {sheet_name}. Available sheets: {', '.join(wb.sheetnames)}"
            ws = wb[sheet_name]
    else:
        ws = wb.active

    rows = []
    for i, row in enumerate(ws.iter_rows(values_only=True)):
        if i >= max_rows:
            rows.append("... (truncated)")
            break
        rows.append("\t".join(str(cell) if cell is not None else "" for cell in row))

    title = ws.title
    visible_rows = min(ws.max_row, max_rows)
    wb.close()
    return f"Sheet: {title}\nRows: {visible_rows}\n\n" + "\n".join(rows)


async def _execute_write_excel(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    import openpyxl

    file_path = Path(args["filePath"])
    try:
        _check_path(file_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    data_str = args["data"]
    sheet_name = args.get("sheet_name", "Sheet1")

    rows_data = []
    if data_str.strip().startswith("|"):
        lines = data_str.strip().split("\n")
        for line in lines:
            if line.strip().startswith("|"):
                parts = [cell.strip() for cell in line.split("|")[1:-1]]
                if parts and all(re.fullmatch(r":?-{3,}:?", cell) for cell in parts):
                    continue
                rows_data.append(parts)
    else:
        parsed = parse_json_value(data_str)
        if parsed:
            if isinstance(parsed, list):
                rows_data = parsed
            elif isinstance(parsed, dict):
                for key, value in parsed.items():
                    if isinstance(value, list):
                        rows_data = value
                        break

    wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = sheet_name

    for row_data in rows_data:
        if isinstance(row_data, list):
            ws.append(row_data)
        elif isinstance(row_data, dict):
            ws.append(list(row_data.values()))

    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    wb.save(file_path)
    wb.close()
    return f"Successfully wrote {len(rows_data)} rows to {file_path}"


async def _execute_shell(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    command = args["command"]
    workdir = args.get("workdir")

    try:
        _check_shell(command)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if workdir and not Path(workdir).exists():
        return f"Error: Working directory not found: {workdir}"

    try:
        cmd_args = shlex.split(command)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: Could not parse command: {e}"

    result = subprocess.run(
        cmd_args,
        shell=False,  # never pass through OS shell — prevents injection via quoted args
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        cwd=workdir,
        timeout=60
    )
    output = result.stdout if result.stdout else result.stderr
    status = f"Exit code: {result.returncode}"
    return f"{status}\n{output}" if output else f"{status}\n(no output)"


async def _execute_glob(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    base_path = Path(args.get("path", "."))
    pattern = args["pattern"]

    try:
        _check_path(base_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if not base_path.exists():
        return f"Error: Search path not found: {base_path}"

    matches = []
    for match in base_path.rglob(pattern):
        matches.append(str(match))

    return "\n".join(matches) if matches else "No files matched"


async def _execute_grep(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    base_path = Path(args.get("path", "."))
    pattern = args["pattern"]
    include = args.get("include")

    try:
        _check_path(base_path)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if not base_path.exists():
        return f"Error: Search path not found: {base_path}"

    results = []
    regex = re.compile(pattern)

    for file_path in base_path.rglob("*"):
        if include and not fnmatch.fnmatch(file_path.name, include):
            continue
        if file_path.is_file():
            try:
                for line_num, line in enumerate(file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n"), 1):
                    if regex.search(line):
                        results.append(f"{file_path}:{line_num}: {line.rstrip()}")
            except Exception:
                continue

    return "\n".join(results) if results else "No matches found"


async def _execute_api_call(args: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    import httpx

    endpoint = args["endpoint"]
    method = args.get("method", "GET").upper()
    body = args.get("body")
    params = args.get("params")

    # LLMs sometimes place request fields (e.g. "data", "operation") at the top
    # level of the tool args instead of nesting them under "body".  Detect this
    # and auto-wrap them so the call doesn't silently send an empty body.
    KNOWN_TOP_LEVEL = {"endpoint", "method", "body", "params"}
    stray_keys = {k: v for k, v in args.items() if k not in KNOWN_TOP_LEVEL}
    if stray_keys and body is None:
        body = stray_keys
        logger.warning(
            "api_call: 'body' was None but found top-level keys %s — auto-wrapping as body",
            list(stray_keys.keys()),
        )

    logger.info("api_call: %s %s body_keys=%s", method, endpoint, list(body.keys()) if isinstance(body, dict) else body)

    try:
        _check_api_endpoint(endpoint)
    except ValueError as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    # Normalise to a relative path — strip any accidental host prefix the model might add
    relative = "/" + endpoint.lstrip("/")

    if _fastapi_app is not None:
        # In-process call via ASGI transport — no network, works in every deployment
        transport = httpx.ASGITransport(app=_fastapi_app)
        base_url = "http://testserver"
    else:
        # Fallback: real HTTP (dev only — set API_BASE_URL to the internal bind address)
        transport = None
        base_url = os.environ.get("API_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:8000")

    url = f"{base_url.rstrip('/')}{relative}"

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport, base_url=base_url, timeout=30.0) as client:
        if method == "GET":
            response = await client.get(url, params=params)
        elif method == "POST":
            response = await client.post(url, json=body, params=params)
        elif method == "PUT":
            response = await client.put(url, json=body, params=params)
        elif method == "DELETE":
            response = await client.delete(url, params=params)
        else:
            return f"Unsupported HTTP method: {method}"

        try:
            return json.dumps(response.json(), indent=2)
        except Exception:
            return response.text if response.text else f"Status: {response.status_code}"


def build_tools_planning_prompt(user_message: str) -> str:
    return f"""You are the tools skill planner.

Read the user's request and determine if any tools should be used (read, write, read_excel, write_excel, shell, glob, grep, api_call).

Return only JSON with this shape:
{{
  "should_use_tools": true,
  "tools": [
    {{"name": "read", "filePath": "path/to/file", "limit": 100, "offset": 1}},
    {{"name": "write", "filePath": "path/to/file", "content": "content to write"}},
    {{"name": "read_excel", "filePath": "path/to/file.xlsx", "sheet": "Sheet1", "max_rows": 100}},
    {{"name": "write_excel", "filePath": "path/to/file.xlsx", "data": "markdown table or JSON", "sheet_name": "Sheet1"}},
    {{"name": "shell", "command": "command to execute", "workdir": "optional directory"}},
    {{"name": "glob", "pattern": "**/*.py"}},
    {{"name": "grep", "pattern": "regex pattern", "path": "optional path", "include": "*.py"}},
    {{"name": "api_call", "endpoint": "/transform/execute", "method": "POST", "body": {{"data": [{{"id": 1, "field": "value"}}], "operation": {{"NewFieldName": "out", "Operation": "Conditional", "Inputs": "field", "comparator": "==", "value": "x", "true_value": "yes", "false_value": "no"}}}}}}
  ]
}}

For api_call:
- endpoint: API path (e.g., '/api/control/run', '/api/health')
- method: GET, POST, PUT, DELETE (default: GET)
- body: JSON object for POST/PUT
- params: URL query parameters

Only include tools that are actually needed. If no tools are needed, set should_use_tools to false and tools to empty array.

User request:
{user_message}"""


def build_tools_system_prompt(skill_plan: str) -> str:
    tools_json = json.dumps(TOOL_DEFINITIONS, indent=2)
    return f"""You have access to the following tools:

{tools_json}

Use this turn-wise:
1. Treat this skill plan as the result of the planning turn:
{skill_plan}
2. Execute the planned tools using the exact JSON format specified
3. Report the results of each tool execution to the user

Tool execution rules:
- Execute tools in the order planned
- Report tool output exactly as returned
- If a tool fails, report the error and continue
- Do not execute unauthorized or potentially harmful commands
- Use absolute paths when possible
- Report success/failure for each tool"""
