CHATSIDEBAR

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, MessageSquare, Trash2, Clock, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChat } from '../lib/chatContext';

const formatDate = (iso) => {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    const now = new Date();
    const diffDays = Math.floor((now - d) / 86400000);
    if (diffDays === 0) return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    if (diffDays === 1) return 'Yesterday';
    if (diffDays < 7) return d.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'short' });
    return d.toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
};

export default function ChatSidebar() {
    const { chats, currentChatId, createNewChat, selectChat, deleteChat } = useChat();
    const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);

    const handleDelete = (chatId, e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        deleteChat(chatId);
    };

    if (collapsed) {
        return (
            <div style={{
                width: '48px',
                minWidth: '48px',
                height: '100%',
                background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, #f8fafc 100%)',
                borderRight: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                alignItems: 'center',
                paddingTop: '14px',
                flexShrink: 0
            }}>
                <button
                    onClick={() => setCollapsed(false)}
                    title="Expand sidebar"
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        width: '34px',
                        height: '34px',
                        backgroundColor: 'white',
                        border: '1px solid #dbe1ea',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        transition: 'all 0.15s'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f9fafb'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#374151'; }}
                    onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'white'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#6b7280'; }}
                >
                    <ChevronRight size={16} />
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <aside style={{
            width: '272px',
            minWidth: '272px',
            height: '100%',
            background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, #f8fafc 100%)',
            borderRight: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            flexShrink: 0,
            overflow: 'hidden'
        }}>
            <div style={{ padding: '14px 14px 10px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                <button
                    onClick={createNewChat}
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        gap: '8px',
                        flex: 1,
                        padding: '11px 16px',
                        background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #7b0012 0%, #db0011 100%)',
                        color: 'white',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        fontWeight: '600',
                        letterSpacing: '0.02em',
                        boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(123,0,18,0.25)',
                        transition: 'all 0.15s'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)'; e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 12px rgba(123,0,18,0.35)'; }}
                    onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)'; e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 2px 8px rgba(123,0,18,0.25)'; }}
                >
                    <Plus size={15} strokeWidth={2.5} /> New Chat
                </button>
                <button
                    onClick={() => setCollapsed(true)}
                    title="Collapse sidebar"
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        width: '34px',
                        height: '34px',
                        backgroundColor: 'white',
                        border: '1px solid #dbe1ea',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        flexShrink: 0,
                        transition: 'all 0.15s'
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f9fafb'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#374151'; }}
                    onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'white'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#6b7280'; }}
                >
                    <ChevronLeft size={16} />
                </button>
            </div>

            <div style={{
                padding: '8px 16px 8px',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '6px',
            }}>
                <Clock size={12} style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }} />
                <span style={{
                    color: '#64748b',
                    fontSize: '11px',
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    textTransform: 'uppercase',
                    letterSpacing: '0.06em'
                }}>
                    Recent Chats
                </span>
            </div>

            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: '0 8px 8px' }}>
                {chats.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        padding: '32px 20px',
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        color: '#94a3b8',
                        fontSize: '13px'
                    }}>
                        <MessageSquare size={32} style={{ marginBottom: '10px', opacity: 0.45 }} />
                        <div style={{ color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>No conversations yet</div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px', opacity: 0.8 }}>Click "New Chat" to begin</div>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    chats.map(chat => {
                        const isActive = currentChatId === chat.id;
                        const msgCount = chat.messages?.length || 0;
                        return (
                            <div
                                key={chat.id}
                                onClick={() => selectChat(chat.id)}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '10px 12px',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    marginBottom: '3px',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    backgroundColor: isActive ? '#fff1f2' : 'transparent',
                                    border: isActive ? '1px solid #fecdd3' : '1px solid transparent',
                                    boxShadow: isActive ? '0 1px 2px rgba(123,0,18,0.08)' : 'none',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '8px',
                                    transition: 'all 0.12s'
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={e => { if (!isActive) e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9'; }}
                                onMouseLeave={e => { if (!isActive) e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; }}
                            >
                                <div style={{
                                    width: '30px',
                                    height: '30px',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    flexShrink: 0,
                                    backgroundColor: isActive ? '#9b0015' : '#e2e8f0',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    justifyContent: 'center',
                                    transition: 'background-color 0.12s'
                                }}>
                                    <MessageSquare size={14} color={isActive ? 'white' : '#64748b'} />
                                </div>

                                <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                                    <div style={{
                                        fontSize: '13px',
                                        fontWeight: isActive ? '700' : '500',
                                        color: isActive ? '#9b0015' : '#334155',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',
                                        textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                        lineHeight: '1.3'
                                    }}>
                                        {chat.title}
                                    </div>
                                    {(() => {
                                        const lastMsg = chat.messages?.[chat.messages.length - 1];
                                        return lastMsg ? (
                                            <div style={{
                                                fontSize: '11.5px',
                                                color: '#94a3b8',
                                                marginTop: '2px',
                                                overflow: 'hidden',
                                                textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                                                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                                lineHeight: '1.4'
                                            }}>
                                                {lastMsg.content}
                                            </div>
                                        ) : null;
                                    })()}
                                    <div style={{
                                        fontSize: '10.5px',
                                        color: '#cbd5e1',
                                        marginTop: '3px',
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '4px'
                                    }}>
                                        <span>{formatDate(chat.createdAt)}</span>
                                        {msgCount > 0 && (
                                            <>
                                                <span style={{ opacity: 0.5 }}>·</span>
                                                <span>{msgCount} msg{msgCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
                                            </>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <button
                                    onClick={e => handleDelete(chat.id, e)}
                                    style={{
                                        backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                                        border: 'none',
                                        color: '#cbd5e1',
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        padding: '4px',
                                        borderRadius: '5px',
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        flexShrink: 0,
                                        transition: 'color 0.15s, background-color 0.15s'
                                    }}
                                    onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.color = '#dc2626'; e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#fee2e2'; }}
                                    onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.color = '#cbd5e1'; e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; }}
                                    title="Delete chat"
                                >
                                    <Trash2 size={13} />
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        );
                    })
                )}
            </div>

            {chats.length > 0 && (
                <div style={{
                    padding: '10px 16px',
                    borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    fontSize: '11px',
                    color: '#94a3b8',
                    textAlign: 'center'
                }}>
                    {chats.length} conversation{chats.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
                </div>
            )}
        </aside>
    );
}
 ==============================

FIEDLDFLOW

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';

const TYPE_STYLES = {
    file: { fill: '#f8fafc', stroke: '#64748b', accent: '#64748b', label: 'File' },
    map: { fill: '#eff6ff', stroke: '#2563eb', accent: '#2563eb', label: 'Map' },
    derive: { fill: '#ecfdf5', stroke: '#059669', accent: '#059669', label: 'Derive' },
    filter: { fill: '#fff7ed', stroke: '#ea580c', accent: '#ea580c', label: 'Filter' },
    calc: { fill: '#fefce8', stroke: '#ca8a04', accent: '#ca8a04', label: 'Calc' },
    lookup: { fill: '#f5f3ff', stroke: '#7c3aed', accent: '#7c3aed', label: 'Lookup' },
    enrich: { fill: '#ecfeff', stroke: '#0891b2', accent: '#0891b2', label: 'Enrich' },
    default: { fill: '#f9fafb', stroke: '#6b7280', accent: '#6b7280', label: 'Step' }
};

const NODE_WIDTH = 238;
const NODE_HEIGHT = 92;
const DETAIL_HEIGHT = 52;
const COLUMN_GAP = 96;
const ROW_GAP = 34;
const PADDING = 28;

const getNodeStyle = (type) => TYPE_STYLES[type] || TYPE_STYLES.default;

const truncate = (value, maxLength) => {
    const text = String(value || '');
    return text.length > maxLength ? `${text.slice(0, maxLength - 1)}...` : text;
};

const buildLayout = (nodes, edges, showDetails) => {
    const ids = new Set(nodes.map((node) => node.id));
    const incoming = new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.id, []]));
    const outgoing = new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.id, []]));

    edges.forEach((edge) => {
        if (!ids.has(edge.from) || !ids.has(edge.to)) return;
        incoming.get(edge.to).push(edge.from);
        outgoing.get(edge.from).push(edge.to);
    });

    const levels = new Map();
    const visiting = new Set();

    const getLevel = (nodeId) => {
        if (levels.has(nodeId)) return levels.get(nodeId);
        if (visiting.has(nodeId)) return 0;

        visiting.add(nodeId);
        const parents = incoming.get(nodeId) || [];
        const level = parents.length === 0
            ? 0
            : Math.max(...parents.map((parentId) => getLevel(parentId) + 1));
        visiting.delete(nodeId);
        levels.set(nodeId, level);
        return level;
    };

    nodes.forEach((node) => getLevel(node.id));

    const columns = [];
    nodes.forEach((node, originalIndex) => {
        const level = levels.get(node.id) || 0;
        if (!columns[level]) columns[level] = [];
        columns[level].push({ ...node, originalIndex });
    });

    columns.forEach((column) => {
        column.sort((a, b) => {
            const aParents = incoming.get(a.id) || [];
            const bParents = incoming.get(b.id) || [];
            const aParentIndex = aParents.length ? nodes.findIndex((node) => node.id === aParents[0]) : a.originalIndex;
            const bParentIndex = bParents.length ? nodes.findIndex((node) => node.id === bParents[0]) : b.originalIndex;
            return aParentIndex - bParentIndex || a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
        });
    });

    const nodeHeight = showDetails ? NODE_HEIGHT + DETAIL_HEIGHT : NODE_HEIGHT;
    const maxRows = Math.max(1, ...columns.map((column) => column.length));
    const width = Math.max(620, PADDING * 2 + columns.length * NODE_WIDTH + Math.max(0, columns.length - 1) * COLUMN_GAP);
    const height = Math.max(220, PADDING * 2 + maxRows * nodeHeight + Math.max(0, maxRows - 1) * ROW_GAP);
    const positions = {};

    columns.forEach((column, columnIndex) => {
        const columnHeight = column.length * nodeHeight + Math.max(0, column.length - 1) * ROW_GAP;
        const startY = PADDING + Math.max(0, (height - PADDING * 2 - columnHeight) / 2);

        column.forEach((node, rowIndex) => {
            positions[node.id] = {
                x: PADDING + columnIndex * (NODE_WIDTH + COLUMN_GAP),
                y: startY + rowIndex * (nodeHeight + ROW_GAP),
                height: nodeHeight
            };
        });
    });

    return { positions, width, height, nodeHeight };
};

const FieldFlowDiagram = ({ config }) => {
    const [showDetails, setShowDetails] = useState(true);
    const [selectedNodeId, setSelectedNodeId] = useState(null);

    const nodes = useMemo(() => Array.isArray(config?.nodes) ? config.nodes : [], [config]);
    const edges = useMemo(() => Array.isArray(config?.edges) ? config.edges : [], [config]);
    const layout = useMemo(() => buildLayout(nodes, edges, showDetails), [nodes, edges, showDetails]);

    if (!config || nodes.length === 0) {
        return (
            <div style={{ padding: '14px', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                No flowchart data available.
            </div>
        );
    }

    const visibleTypes = [...new Set(nodes.map((node) => node.type || 'default'))];

    return (
        <div style={{
            border: '1px solid #dbe3ef',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
            marginBottom: '16px',
            overflow: 'hidden'
        }}>
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                gap: '12px',
                padding: '12px 14px',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc'
            }}>
                <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                        {config.title || 'Field Flow'}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                        {nodes.length} nodes · {edges.length} links
                    </div>
                </div>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowDetails((value) => !value)}
                    style={{
                        display: 'inline-flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        height: '30px',
                        padding: '0 10px',
                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        color: '#334155',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                    }}
                    title={showDetails ? 'Hide node details' : 'Show node details'}
                >
                    {showDetails ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                    {showDetails ? 'Details' : 'Summary'}
                </button>
            </div>

            <div style={{ overflow: 'auto', backgroundColor: '#fbfdff' }}>
                <svg
                    width={layout.width}
                    height={layout.height}
                    viewBox={`0 0 ${layout.width} ${layout.height}`}
                    role="img"
                    aria-label={config.title || 'Field flow diagram'}
                    style={{ display: 'block', minWidth: '100%' }}
                >
                    <defs>
                        <marker id="field-flow-arrow" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" refX="8.5" refY="5" orient="auto">
                            <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#94a3b8" />
                        </marker>
                    </defs>

                    {edges.map((edge, index) => {
                        const from = layout.positions[edge.from];
                        const to = layout.positions[edge.to];
                        if (!from || !to) return null;

                        const sourceNode = nodes.find((node) => node.id === edge.from);
                        const style = getNodeStyle(sourceNode?.type);
                        const x1 = from.x + NODE_WIDTH;
                        const y1 = from.y + from.height / 2;
                        const x2 = to.x;
                        const y2 = to.y + to.height / 2;
                        const midX = x1 + Math.max(38, (x2 - x1) / 2);

                        return (
                            <g key={`${edge.from}-${edge.to}-${index}`}>
                                <path
                                    d={`M ${x1} ${y1} C ${midX} ${y1}, ${midX} ${y2}, ${x2 - 10} ${y2}`}
                                    fill="none"
                                    stroke={style.accent}
                                    strokeOpacity="0.55"
                                    strokeWidth="2"
                                    markerEnd="url(#field-flow-arrow)"
                                />
                                {edge.label && (
                                    <text x={midX + 8} y={Math.min(y1, y2) + Math.abs(y2 - y1) / 2 - 6} fill="#475569" fontSize="11">
                                        {truncate(edge.label, 24)}
                                    </text>
                                )}
                            </g>
                        );
                    })}

                    {nodes.map((node) => {
                        const position = layout.positions[node.id];
                        const style = getNodeStyle(node.type);
                        const isSelected = selectedNodeId === node.id;
                        const details = Array.isArray(node.details) ? node.details.slice(0, 3) : [];

                        return (
                            <g
                                key={node.id}
                                transform={`translate(${position.x}, ${position.y})`}
                                onClick={() => setSelectedNodeId(isSelected ? null : node.id)}
                                style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                <rect
                                    x="0"
                                    y="0"
                                    width={NODE_WIDTH}
                                    height={position.height}
                                    rx="8"
                                    fill={style.fill}
                                    stroke={isSelected ? '#0f172a' : style.stroke}
                                    strokeWidth={isSelected ? 2.4 : 1.4}
                                />
                                <rect x="0" y="0" width="5" height={position.height} rx="4" fill={style.accent} />
                                <text x="16" y="24" fill="#64748b" fontSize="10" fontWeight="700" letterSpacing="0">
                                    {style.label.toUpperCase()}
                                </text>
                                <text x="16" y="50" fill="#0f172a" fontSize="13" fontWeight="700">
                                    {truncate(node.label || node.id, 28)}
                                </text>
                                <text x="16" y="70" fill="#64748b" fontSize="11">
                                    {truncate(node.id, 34)}
                                </text>

                                {showDetails && details.map((detail, detailIndex) => (
                                    <text key={detailIndex} x="16" y={102 + detailIndex * 16} fill="#334155" fontSize="11">
                                        {truncate(detail, 34)}
                                    </text>
                                ))}
                                {showDetails && Array.isArray(node.details) && node.details.length > 3 && (
                                    <text x="16" y="150" fill="#64748b" fontSize="11">
                                        +{node.details.length - 3} more details
                                    </text>
                                )}
                            </g>
                        );
                    })}
                </svg>
            </div>

            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                gap: '8px',
                padding: '10px 14px',
                borderTop: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff'
            }}>
                {visibleTypes.map((type) => {
                    const style = getNodeStyle(type);
                    return (
                        <div key={type} style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '11px', color: '#475569' }}>
                            <span style={{ width: '10px', height: '10px', borderRadius: '2px', backgroundColor: style.accent }} />
                            {style.label}
                        </div>
                    );
                })}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default FieldFlowDiagram;

MARKDOWNRENDERE

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize';
import { Prism as SyntaxHighlighter } from 'react-syntax-highlighter';
import { oneDark } from 'react-syntax-highlighter/dist/esm/styles/prism';
import { Copy, Check, ExternalLink, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import {
    Bar,
    BarChart,
    CartesianGrid,
    Cell,
    Legend,
    Line,
    LineChart,
    Pie,
    PieChart,
    ResponsiveContainer,
    Tooltip,
    XAxis,
    YAxis
} from 'recharts';
import FieldFlowDiagram from './FieldFlowDiagram';

const CHART_COLORS = ['#1e40af', '#2563eb', '#16a34a', '#d97706', '#7c3aed', '#0891b2'];

const ArtifactButton = ({ children, onClick, title }) => (
    <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClick}
        title={title}
        style={{
            display: 'inline-flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '6px',
            height: '30px',
            padding: '0 10px',
            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
            borderRadius: '6px',
            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
            color: '#334155',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: '12px',
            fontWeight: 600
        }}
    >
        {children}
    </button>
);

const HtmlArtifactBlock = ({ children }) => {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
    const html = String(children).replace(/\n$/, '');

    const copyArtifact = async () => {
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(html);
            setCopied(true);
            setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to copy HTML artifact:', error);
        }
    };

    const openArtifact = () => {
        const blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
        setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 30000);
    };

    return (
        <div style={{
            border: '1px solid #dbe3ef',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
            marginBottom: '16px',
            overflow: 'hidden'
        }}>
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                gap: '12px',
                padding: '10px 12px',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc'
            }}>
                <div>
                    <div style={{ color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700 }}>
                        HTML Artifact
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '11px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                        Sandboxed preview - inline assets only
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <ArtifactButton
                        onClick={() => setExpanded((value) => !value)}
                        title={expanded ? 'Use compact preview height' : 'Use larger preview height'}
                    >
                        {expanded ? <Minimize2 size={14} /> : <Maximize2 size={14} />}
                        {expanded ? 'Compact' : 'Larger'}
                    </ArtifactButton>
                    <ArtifactButton onClick={copyArtifact} title="Copy artifact HTML">
                        {copied ? <Check size={14} /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                        {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
                    </ArtifactButton>
                    <ArtifactButton onClick={openArtifact} title="Open artifact in a new tab">
                        <ExternalLink size={14} />
                        Open
                    </ArtifactButton>
                </div>
            </div>
            <iframe
                title="HTML artifact preview"
                srcDoc={html}
                sandbox="allow-scripts allow-forms"
                referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                loading="lazy"
                style={{
                    display: 'block',
                    width: '100%',
                    height: expanded ? '760px' : '560px',
                    border: 0,
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff'
                }}
            />
        </div>
    );
};

const Flow3DBlock = ({ children }) => {
    let config;
    let parseError = null;

    try {
        config = JSON.parse(String(children).replace(/\n$/, ''));
    } catch (error) {
        parseError = error;
    }

    if (parseError) {
        return (
            <div style={{ padding: '12px', border: '1px solid #fecaca', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#1e3a8a' }}>
                Unable to render flowchart. The flow JSON is invalid.
            </div>
        );
    }

    return <FieldFlowDiagram config={config} />;
};

const FlowBlock = ({ children }) => {
    let config;
    let parseError = null;

    try {
        config = JSON.parse(String(children).replace(/\n$/, ''));
    } catch (error) {
        parseError = error;
    }

    if (parseError) {
        return (
            <div style={{ padding: '12px', border: '1px solid #fecaca', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#1e3a8a' }}>
                Unable to render flowchart. The flow JSON is invalid.

};

const ChartBlock = ({ children }) => {
    let config;
    let parseError = null;

    try {
        config = JSON.parse(String(children).replace(/\n$/, ''));
    } catch (error) {
        parseError = error;
    }

    if (parseError) {
        return (
            <div style={{ padding: '12px', border: '1px solid #fecaca', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#1e3a8a' }}>
                Unable to render chart. The chart JSON is invalid.
            </div>
        );
    }

    const data = Array.isArray(config.data) ? config.data : [];
    const chartType = config.type || 'bar';
    const xKey = config.xKey || 'name';
    const yKeys = Array.isArray(config.yKeys) && config.yKeys.length > 0
        ? config.yKeys
        : Object.keys(data[0] || {}).filter((key) => key !== xKey && typeof data[0]?.[key] === 'number');

    if (data.length === 0 || yKeys.length === 0) {
        return (
            <div style={{ padding: '12px', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#666' }}>
                No chart data available.
            </div>
        );
    }

    const renderChart = () => {
        if (chartType === 'line') {
            return (
                <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 16, right: 24, left: 8, bottom: 20 }}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />
                    <XAxis dataKey={xKey} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                    <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                    <Tooltip />
                    <Legend />
                    {yKeys.map((key, index) => (
                        <Line key={key} type="monotone" dataKey={key} stroke={CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length]} strokeWidth={2} dot={{ r: 3 }} />
                    ))}
                </LineChart>
            );
        }

        if (chartType === 'pie') {
            const valueKey = yKeys[0];
            return (
                <PieChart margin={{ top: 16, right: 24, left: 8, bottom: 20 }}>
                    <Tooltip />
                    <Legend />
                    <Pie data={data} dataKey={valueKey} nameKey={xKey} outerRadius="75%" label>
                        {data.map((_, index) => (
                            <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length]} />
                        ))}
                    </Pie>
                </PieChart>
            );
        }

        return (
            <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 16, right: 24, left: 8, bottom: 20 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e7eb" />
                <XAxis dataKey={xKey} tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                <Tooltip />
                <Legend />
                {yKeys.map((key, index) => (
                    <Bar key={key} dataKey={key} fill={CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length]} radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                ))}
            </BarChart>
        );
    };

    return (
        <div style={{
            border: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            backgroundColor: 'white',
            padding: '14px',
            marginBottom: '16px'
        }}>
            {config.title && (
                <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#111827', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                    {config.title}
                </div>
            )}
            <div style={{ width: '100%', height: config.height || 320 }}>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    {renderChart()}
                </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const CodeBlock = ({ language, children, ...props }) => {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    const codeString = String(children).replace(/\n$/, '');

    if (language === 'chart-json') {
        return <ChartBlock>{children}</ChartBlock>;
    }

    if (language === 'html-artifact') {
        return <HtmlArtifactBlock>{children}</HtmlArtifactBlock>;
    }

    if (language === 'flow-json') {
        return <FlowBlock>{children}</FlowBlock>;
    }

    if (language === 'flow-3d') {
        return <Flow3DBlock>{children}</Flow3DBlock>;
    }

    if (language === 'json') {
        try {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(codeString);
            if (Array.isArray(parsed.nodes) && Array.isArray(parsed.edges)) {
                return <FlowBlock>{children}</FlowBlock>;
            }
            if (Array.isArray(parsed.data) && (parsed.type || parsed.xKey || parsed.yKeys)) {
                return <ChartBlock>{children}</ChartBlock>;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            // Keep invalid or ordinary JSON rendered as a normal code block.
        }
    }

    const handleCopy = async () => {
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(codeString);
            setCopied(true);
            setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Failed to copy:', err);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
            {/* Header bar */}
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
                backgroundColor: '#2d2d2d',
                padding: '8px 12px',
                borderRadius: '8px 8px 0 0',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #444'
            }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#999', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
                    {language || 'code'}
                </span>
                <button
                    onClick={handleCopy}
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '4px',
                        backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                        border: 'none',
                        color: copied ? '#22c55e' : '#999',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        padding: '4px 8px',
                        borderRadius: '4px',
                        transition: 'all 0.2s'
                    }}
                >
                    {copied ? <Check size={14} /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                    {copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy'}
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Code block */}
            <SyntaxHighlighter
                style={oneDark}
                language={language}
                PreTag="div"
                customStyle={{
                    margin: 0,
                    borderRadius: '0 0 8px 8px',
                    fontSize: '13px'
                }}
                {...props}
            >
                {codeString}
            </SyntaxHighlighter>
        </div>
    );
};

export default function MarkdownRenderer({ content }) {
    return (
        <div className="markdown-content" style={{ lineHeight: '1.7' }}>
            <ReactMarkdown
                remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
                rehypePlugins={[rehypeSanitize]}
                components={{
                    code({ node, inline, className, children, ...props }) {
                        const match = /language-([\w-]+)/.exec(className || '');
                        return !inline && match ? (
                            <CodeBlock language={match[1]} {...props}>
                                {children}
                            </CodeBlock>
                        ) : (
                            <code
                                style={{
                                    backgroundColor: '#f5f5f5',
                                    padding: '2px 6px',
                                    borderRadius: '4px',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    color: '#1e40af'
                                }}
                                {...props}
                            >
                                {children}
                            </code>
                        );
                    },
                    table({ children }) {
                        return (
                            <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                <table style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    borderCollapse: 'collapse',
                                    fontSize: '14px'
                                }}>
                                    {children}
                                </table>
                            </div>
                        );
                    },
                    th({ children }) {
                        return (
                            <th style={{
                                backgroundColor: '#f5f5f5',
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                textAlign: 'left',
                                borderBottom: '2px solid #ddd',
                                fontWeight: '600'
                            }}>
                                {children}
                            </th>
                        );
                    },
                    td({ children }) {
                        return (
                            <td style={{
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                borderBottom: '1px solid #eee'
                            }}>
                                {children}
                            </td>
                        );
                    },
                    blockquote({ children }) {
                        return (
                            <blockquote style={{
                                borderLeft: '4px solid #1e40af',
                                paddingLeft: '16px',
                                margin: '16px 0',
                                color: '#666',
                                fontStyle: 'italic'
                            }}>
                                {children}
                            </blockquote>
                        );
                    },
                    h1({ children }) {
                        return <h1 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: '700', margin: '20px 0 12px', color: '#333' }}>{children}</h1>;
                    },
                    h2({ children }) {
                        return <h2 style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '600', margin: '18px 0 10px', color: '#333' }}>{children}</h2>;
                    },
                    h3({ children }) {
                        return <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '600', margin: '16px 0 8px', color: '#333' }}>{children}</h3>;
                    },
                    ul({ children }) {
                        return <ul style={{ paddingLeft: '24px', margin: '12px 0' }}>{children}</ul>;
                    },
                    ol({ children }) {
                        return <ol style={{ paddingLeft: '24px', margin: '12px 0' }}>{children}</ol>;
                    },
                    li({ children }) {
                        return <li style={{ marginBottom: '6px' }}>{children}</li>;
                    },
                    p({ children }) {
                        return <p style={{ margin: '12px 0' }}>{children}</p>;
                    },
                    a({ children, href }) {
                        return (
                            <a
                                href={href}
                                target="_blank"
                                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                                style={{ color: '#1e40af', textDecoration: 'underline' }}
                            >
                                {children}
                            </a>
                        );
                    }
                }}
            >
                {content}
            </ReactMarkdown>
        </div>
    );
}

MESSAGE BUBBLE

import { useState } from 'react';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { Copy, Check, Bot, User } from 'lucide-react';
import MarkdownRenderer from './MarkdownRenderer';

export default function MessageBubble({ message }) {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);
    const isUser = message.role === 'user';

    const timeAgo = message.timestamp
        ? formatDistanceToNow(new Date(message.timestamp), { addSuffix: true })
        : 'Just now';

    const copyMessage = async () => {
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(message.content);
            setCopied(true);
            setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Failed to copy:', err);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div
            onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(true)}
            onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(false)}
            style={{
                display: 'flex',
                gap: '14px',
                padding: '16px 20px',
                marginBottom: '10px',
                backgroundColor: isUser ? '#f8fafc' : 'white',
                borderRadius: '10px',
                border: isUser ? '1px solid #e2e8f0' : '1px solid #fecdd3',
                borderLeft: isUser ? '3px solid #64748b' : '3px solid #db0011',
                boxShadow: hovered
                    ? '0 8px 24px rgba(15,23,42,0.09)'
                    : '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)',
                position: 'relative',
                transition: 'box-shadow 0.15s'
            }}
        >
            {/* Avatar */}
            <div style={{
                width: '38px',
                height: '38px',
                borderRadius: '10px',
                background: isUser
                    ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #374151, #1f2937)'
                    : 'linear-gradient(135deg, #7b0012, #db0011)',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                flexShrink: 0,
                boxShadow: isUser
                    ? '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)'
                    : '0 2px 6px rgba(123,0,18,0.25)'
            }}>
                {isUser
                    ? <User color="white" size={17} />
                    : <Bot color="white" size={19} />
                }
            </div>

            {/* Content */}
            <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                {/* Header row */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    marginBottom: '7px'
                }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <span style={{
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            color: isUser ? '#1f2937' : '#9b0015'
                        }}>
                            {isUser ? 'You' : 'AI Assistant'}
                        </span>
                        <span style={{
                            fontSize: '11px',
                            color: '#9ca3af',
                            fontWeight: '400'
                        }}>
                            {timeAgo}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Copy button — appears on hover */}
                    <button
                        onClick={copyMessage}
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '4px',
                            backgroundColor: copied ? '#f0fdf4' : '#f9fafb',
                            border: `1px solid ${copied ? '#bbf7d0' : '#e5e7eb'}`,
                            borderRadius: '6px',
                            color: copied ? '#16a34a' : '#6b7280',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: '3px 8px',
                            fontSize: '11px',
                            fontWeight: '500',
                            transition: 'all 0.15s',
                            opacity: hovered || copied ? 1 : 0.35,
                        }}
                        title="Copy message"
                    >
                        {copied ? <Check size={11} /> : <Copy size={11} />}
                        {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Message body */}
                <div style={{
                    color: '#1f2937',
                    fontSize: '14.5px',
                    lineHeight: '1.75',
                    wordBreak: 'break-word'
                }}>
                    {isUser ? (
                        <div style={{ whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' }}>{message.content}</div>
                    ) : (
                        <MarkdownRenderer content={message.content} />
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

MESSAGEINPUT

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Send, Code, Square } from 'lucide-react';
import { useChat } from '../lib/chatContext';

export default function MessageInput({ onSendMessage, isLoading, disabled = false, onStop, pendingApproval = false }) {
    const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
    const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);
    const textareaRef = useRef(null);
    const { queryLastResponse, setQueryLastResponse } = useChat();

    const canSend = message.trim() && !disabled && !isLoading;

    const handleSend = () => {
        if (!canSend) return;
        onSendMessage(message.trim());
        setMessage('');
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
            handleSend();
        }
    };

    const insertCodeBlock = () => {
        const textarea = textareaRef.current;
        if (!textarea) return;

        const start = textarea.selectionStart;
        const end = textarea.selectionEnd;
        const codeBlock = '```\n\n```';
        const newValue = message.slice(0, start) + codeBlock + message.slice(end);
        setMessage(newValue);

        setTimeout(() => {
            textarea.focus();
            textarea.setSelectionRange(start + 4, start + 4);
        }, 0);
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        const textarea = textareaRef.current;
        if (textarea) {
            textarea.style.height = 'auto';
            textarea.style.height = Math.min(textarea.scrollHeight, 150) + 'px';
        }
    }, [message]);

    return (
        <div style={{
            padding: '14px 24px 16px',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.96)',
            borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
            boxShadow: '0 -10px 26px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)'
        }}>
            <div style={{ width: '100%' }}>
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    gap: '10px',
                    alignItems: 'flex-end',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '10px',
                    border: `1.5px solid ${focused ? '#db0011' : '#cbd5e1'}`,
                    padding: '10px 12px 10px 16px',
                    transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
                    boxShadow: focused ? '0 0 0 3px rgba(219,0,17,0.14)' : '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,0.05)'
                }}>
                    <textarea
                        ref={textareaRef}
                        value={message}
                        onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
                        onFocus={() => setFocused(true)}
                        onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
                        placeholder={disabled ? 'Backend not available...' : pendingApproval ? 'Approve or reject the tool call above to continue…' : 'Message Config Assistant... (Shift+Enter for new line)'}
                        disabled={isLoading || disabled}
                        style={{
                            flex: 1,
                            backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                            color: '#1f2937',
                            border: 'none',
                            fontSize: '14.5px',
                            resize: 'none',
                            minHeight: '24px',
                            maxHeight: '150px',
                            outline: 'none',
                            fontFamily: 'inherit',
                            lineHeight: '1.6',
                            opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
                            padding: '2px 0'
                        }}
                        rows={1}
                    />

                    {isLoading ? (
                        <button
                            onClick={onStop}
                            title="Stop generation"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                flexShrink: 0,
                                background: '#dc2626',
                                color: 'white',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                transition: 'background 0.15s'
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = '#b91c1c'}
                            onMouseLeave={e => e.currentTarget.style.background = '#dc2626'}
                        >
                            <Square size={14} fill="white" />
                        </button>
                    ) : (
                        <button
                            onClick={handleSend}
                            disabled={!canSend}
                            title="Send message (Enter)"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                flexShrink: 0,
                                background: canSend ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #7b0012, #db0011)' : '#e5e7eb',
                                color: canSend ? 'white' : '#9ca3af',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                cursor: canSend ? 'pointer' : 'default',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                transition: 'all 0.15s',
                                boxShadow: canSend ? '0 2px 8px rgba(123,0,18,0.28)' : 'none'
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={e => canSend && (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.06)')}
                            onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1)')}
                        >
                            <Send size={15} />
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>

                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    marginTop: '8px',
                    padding: '0 2px'
                }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={insertCodeBlock}
                            disabled={disabled || isLoading}
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '5px',
                                backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                padding: '5px 10px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                color: '#64748b',
                                cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                fontWeight: '500',
                                transition: 'all 0.15s'
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={e => { if (!disabled) { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#cbd5e1'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#334155'; }}}
                            onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e2e8f0'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#64748b'; }}
                        >
                            <Code size={12} /> Code
                        </button>

                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '500' }}>
                                Query last:
                            </span>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setQueryLastResponse(!queryLastResponse)}
                                title={queryLastResponse
                                    ? 'Enabled - only last AI response sent as context'
                                    : 'Disabled - full conversation history sent'}
                                style={{
                                    background: queryLastResponse ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #7b0012, #db0011)' : '#e5e7eb',
                                    color: queryLastResponse ? 'white' : '#6b7280',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '5px',
                                    padding: '3px 9px',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    fontWeight: '700',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    transition: 'all 0.15s',
                                    letterSpacing: '0.04em'
                                }}
                            >
                                {queryLastResponse ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                        {/* Char count */}
                        {message.length > 0 && (
                            <span style={{
                                fontSize: '11px',
                                color: message.length > 3000 ? '#db0011' : '#94a3b8',
                                fontWeight: message.length > 3000 ? '600' : '400'
                            }}>
                                {message.length.toLocaleString()} chars
                            </span>
                        )}

                        {/* Context mode pill */}
                        <div style={{
                            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px',
                            padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '20px',
                            backgroundColor: queryLastResponse ? '#fff1f2' : '#f1f5f9',
                            border: `1px solid ${queryLastResponse ? '#fecdd3' : '#e2e8f0'}`
                        }}>
                            <span style={{ width: '5px', height: '5px', borderRadius: '50%', backgroundColor: queryLastResponse ? '#db0011' : '#94a3b8', display: 'inline-block', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '10.5px', color: queryLastResponse ? '#9b0015' : '#64748b', fontWeight: '600', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                {queryLastResponse ? 'Last response' : 'Full history'}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                            <kbd style={{
                                padding: '1px 5px',
                                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                borderRadius: '4px',
                                fontSize: '10px',
                                fontFamily: 'inherit'
                            }}>
                                Shift+Enter
                            </kbd>
                            <span>new line</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
