ToolApprovalCard.JS

import { useState } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, Wrench, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';

const TOOL_ICONS = {
    read: '📄',
    write: '✏️',
    read_excel: '📊',
    write_excel: '📊',
    shell: '💻',
    glob: '🔍',
    grep: '🔎',
    api_call: '🌐',
};

const pill = (color, text) => (
    <span style={{
        display: 'inline-block',
        padding: '2px 8px',
        borderRadius: '20px',
        fontSize: '11px',
        fontWeight: 700,
        backgroundColor: color === 'green' ? '#dcfce7' : color === 'red' ? '#fee2e2' : '#fef9c3',
        color: color === 'green' ? '#166534' : color === 'red' ? '#991b1b' : '#854d0e',
    }}>
        {text}
    </span>
);

export default function ToolApprovalCard({ toolName, toolArgs, toolLabel, onApprove, onReject, status = 'pending', isExecuting = false }) {
    const [userInput, setUserInput] = useState('');
    const [showArgs, setShowArgs] = useState(true);
    const [showInput, setShowInput] = useState(false);

    const icon = TOOL_ICONS[toolName] || '🔧';
    const argsJson = JSON.stringify(toolArgs, null, 2);

    const handleApprove = () => onApprove(userInput);
    const handleReject = () => onReject(userInput);

    return (
        <div style={{
            border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
            borderLeft: `3px solid ${status === 'approved' ? '#22c55e' : status === 'rejected' ? '#ef4444' : '#f59e0b'}`,
            borderRadius: '10px',
            backgroundColor: 'white',
            marginBottom: '12px',
            overflow: 'hidden',
            boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)',
        }}>
            {/* Header */}
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '10px',
                padding: '12px 16px',
                backgroundColor: '#fafafa',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
            }}>
                <div style={{
                    width: '32px',
                    height: '32px',
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                    backgroundColor: '#fff7ed',
                    border: '1px solid #fed7aa',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    fontSize: '16px',
                    flexShrink: 0,
                }}>
                    {icon}
                </div>

                <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                            Tool Call: <code style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', color: '#7c3aed' }}>{toolName}</code>
                        </span>
                        {status === 'pending' && !isExecuting && pill('yellow', 'Awaiting Approval')}
                        {status === 'pending' && isExecuting && pill('yellow', 'Executing…')}
                        {status === 'approved' && pill('green', 'Approved')}
                        {status === 'rejected' && pill('red', 'Rejected')}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', marginTop: '2px' }}>{toolLabel}</div>
                </div>

                {status === 'pending' && (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                        <Wrench size={14} color="#f59e0b" />
                    </div>
                )}
                {status === 'approved' && <CheckCircle size={18} color="#22c55e" />}
                {status === 'rejected' && <XCircle size={18} color="#ef4444" />}
            </div>

            {/* Arguments */}
            <div style={{ padding: '0 16px' }}>
                <button
                    onClick={() => setShowArgs(v => !v)}
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        background: 'none',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        padding: '10px 0',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: '#475569',
                        width: '100%',
                        textAlign: 'left',
                    }}
                >
                    {showArgs ? <ChevronUp size={13} /> : <ChevronDown size={13} />}
                    Arguments
                </button>

                {showArgs && (
                    <pre style={{
                        margin: '0 0 12px',
                        padding: '10px 12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#0f172a',
                        color: '#e2e8f0',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        lineHeight: '1.6',
                        overflowX: 'auto',
                        fontFamily: '"Fira Code", "Cascadia Code", Consolas, monospace',
                        whiteSpace: 'pre',
                    }}>
                        {argsJson}
                    </pre>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* User response input + action buttons — only when pending */}
            {status === 'pending' && !isExecuting && (
                <div style={{ padding: '0 16px 14px' }}>
                    {/* Toggle user response */}
                    <button
                        onClick={() => setShowInput(v => !v)}
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '6px',
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: '4px 0 8px',
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            color: '#64748b',
                        }}
                    >
                        <MessageSquare size={12} />
                        {showInput ? 'Hide response field' : 'Add a response (optional)'}
                    </button>

                    {showInput && (
                        <textarea
                            value={userInput}
                            onChange={e => setUserInput(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Add context, a clarification, or a rejection reason…"
                            rows={2}
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                padding: '8px 10px',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontFamily: 'inherit',
                                resize: 'vertical',
                                outline: 'none',
                                marginBottom: '10px',
                                color: '#1f2937',
                                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                            }}
                            onFocus={e => (e.target.style.borderColor = '#db0011')}
                            onBlur={e => (e.target.style.borderColor = '#cbd5e1')}
                        />
                    )}

                    {/* Action buttons */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={handleApprove}
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '6px',
                                padding: '8px 16px',
                                backgroundColor: '#16a34a',
                                color: 'white',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '7px',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                transition: 'background-color 0.15s',
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#15803d')}
                            onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#16a34a')}
                        >
                            <CheckCircle size={14} /> Approve
                        </button>

                        <button
                            onClick={handleReject}
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '6px',
                                padding: '8px 16px',
                                backgroundColor: 'white',
                                color: '#dc2626',
                                border: '1px solid #fca5a5',
                                borderRadius: '7px',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                transition: 'all 0.15s',
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#fee2e2'; e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#f87171'; }}
                            onMouseLeave={e => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'white'; e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#fca5a5'; }}
                        >
                            <XCircle size={14} /> Reject
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Executing state */}
            {status === 'pending' && isExecuting && (
                <div style={{ padding: '0 16px 14px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                    {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
                        <div key={i} style={{
                            width: '6px', height: '6px', borderRadius: '50%',
                            backgroundColor: '#f59e0b',
                            animation: `bounce 1.3s ease-in-out ${i * 0.18}s infinite`
                        }} />
                    ))}
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>Running tool…</span>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* User input shown after resolution */}
            {status !== 'pending' && userInput && (
                <div style={{ padding: '0 16px 12px' }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', marginBottom: '4px' }}>Your response:</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#475569', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>{userInput}</div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

=======================================

CHATCONTEXT

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'config-assistant-chats';

// Generate unique ID
const generateId = () => `chat-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;

// Load/Save helpers
const loadFromStorage = () => {
    try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : { chats: [], currentChatId: null };
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading chats:', e);
        return { chats: [], currentChatId: null };
    }
};

const saveToStorage = (data) => {
    try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error saving chats:', e);
    }
};

// Context
const ChatContext = createContext(null);

export function ChatProvider({ children }) {
    const [chats, setChats] = useState([]);
    const [currentChatId, setCurrentChatId] = useState(null);
    const [currentMessages, setCurrentMessages] = useState([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [streamingContent, setStreamingContent] = useState('');
    const [model, setModel] = useState('gpt-4o-mini');
    const [temperature, setTemperature] = useState(0.7);
    const [environment, setEnvironment] = useState('dev');
    const [queryLastResponse, setQueryLastResponse] = useState(false);

    // Load from storage on mount
    useEffect(() => {
        const data = loadFromStorage();
        setChats(data.chats);
        if (data.currentChatId && data.chats.find(c => c.id === data.currentChatId)) {
            setCurrentChatId(data.currentChatId);
            const chat = data.chats.find(c => c.id === data.currentChatId);
            setCurrentMessages(chat?.messages || []);
        }
    }, []);

    // Save to storage on change
    useEffect(() => {
        saveToStorage({ chats, currentChatId });
    }, [chats, currentChatId]);

    // Create new chat
    const createNewChat = useCallback(() => {
        const newChat = {
            id: generateId(),
            title: 'New Chat',
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            messages: []
        };
        setChats(prev => [newChat, ...prev]);
        setCurrentChatId(newChat.id);
        setCurrentMessages([]);
        return newChat.id;
    }, []);

    // Select chat
    const selectChat = useCallback((chatId) => {
        setCurrentChatId(chatId);
        const chat = chats.find(c => c.id === chatId);
        setCurrentMessages(chat?.messages || []);
    }, [chats]);

    // Delete chat
    const deleteChat = useCallback((chatId) => {
        setChats(prev => prev.filter(c => c.id !== chatId));
        if (currentChatId === chatId) {
            setCurrentChatId(null);
            setCurrentMessages([]);
        }
    }, [currentChatId]);

    // Update current chat messages
    const updateCurrentMessages = useCallback((updater) => {
        setCurrentMessages(prev => {
            const newMessages = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(prev) : updater;
            // Also update in chats array
            setChats(chats => chats.map(chat => {
                if (chat.id === currentChatId) {
                    // Update title from first user message
                    const firstUserMsg = newMessages.find(m => m.role === 'user');
                    const title = firstUserMsg
                        ? firstUserMsg.content.slice(0, 40) + (firstUserMsg.content.length > 40 ? '...' : '')
                        : chat.title;
                    return { ...chat, messages: newMessages, title };
                }
                return chat;
            }));
            return newMessages;
        });
    }, [currentChatId]);

    // Clear current chat
    const clearCurrentChat = useCallback(() => {
        setCurrentMessages([]);
        setChats(prev => prev.map(chat => {
            if (chat.id === currentChatId) {
                return { ...chat, messages: [], title: 'New Chat' };
            }
            return chat;
        }));
    }, [currentChatId]);

    // Export chat
    const exportCurrentChat = useCallback(() => {
        const chatData = {
            messages: currentMessages,
            model,
            temperature,
            exportedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(chatData, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = url;
        link.download = `chat-export-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }, [currentMessages, model, temperature]);

    const value = {
        chats,
        currentChatId,
        currentMessages,
        isLoading,
        setIsLoading,
        streamingContent,
        setStreamingContent,
        model,
        setModel,
        temperature,
        setTemperature,
        environment,
        setEnvironment,
        queryLastResponse,
        setQueryLastResponse,
        createNewChat,
        selectChat,
        deleteChat,
        updateCurrentMessages,
        clearCurrentChat,
        exportCurrentChat
    };

    return (
        <ChatContext.Provider value={value}>
            {children}
        </ChatContext.Provider>
    );
}

export function useChat() {
    const context = useContext(ChatContext);
    if (!context) {
        throw new Error('useChat must be used within a ChatProvider');
    }
    return context;
}

# Test Prompts for Config Assistant & AI Assistant

Two Excel files are in this folder:
- `trade_exceptions.xlsx` — 15 trade-level exception records across EMIR/MiFID2
- `control_config.xlsx` — 12 control definitions, 10 quality rules, 6 enrichment mappings

All prompts assume the backend's working directory or AI_ALLOWED_PATHS covers:
`D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\`

---

## Example 1 — Data Analysis (trade_exceptions.xlsx)

**Use in: AI Assistant or Config Assistant**

### Prompt
```
Read the file D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\trade_exceptions.xlsx and give me:
1. A breakdown of exceptions by type showing count, open vs resolved
2. Which assignees have the most open High severity exceptions
3. Any counterparties appearing in more than one exception

Then write a short markdown summary of the top 3 risks.
```

**What this tests:**
- `read_excel` tool (Sheet: Exception Report)
- Multi-step reasoning over tabular data
- Markdown output formatting

**Config Assistant specific:** You will see a `tool_approval_required` card appear before the file is read. You can approve it (and optionally add a note like "focus on EMIR only") or reject it. After approval the assistant reads the file and continues.

---

## Example 2 — Config Generation (control_config.xlsx)

**Use in: Config Assistant** (tool approval flow is most visible here)

### Prompt
```
Read D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\control_config.xlsx — specifically the "Control Definitions" and "Quality Rules" sheets.

Based on what you find, generate a new completeness control configuration for a new "FX Forward EMIR" control that follows the same pattern as CTRL-001 (FX Spot Completeness). Give it the next available Control ID.

Then write the new control row back to the Excel file in the Control Definitions sheet.
```

**What this tests:**
- `read_excel` tool (2 separate reads — one per sheet)
- Config generation from an existing pattern
- `write_excel` tool to save the new row back

**Config Assistant approval flow:**
1. First approval card: `read_excel` for "Control Definitions" — approve
2. Second approval card: `read_excel` for "Quality Rules" — approve (or add user note: "only show active rules")
3. Third approval card: `write_excel` to save the new row — you can review the exact data that will be written before approving

---

## Example 3 — Formula Design, Live Testing via Transform Validator API, then Config Write

**Use in: Config Assistant** (4–6 approval cards — best demo of the flow)
**Also works in: AI Assistant** (runs all tools automatically)

### Background
The Transform Validator backend exposes `POST /transform/execute`.
Each call takes a `data` array (sample rows) and an `operation` object describing the transform.
Supported `Operation` types include: `Formula`, `Concatenate`, `DateExtract`, `Conditional`, `MapValues`, `StringOperation`, `NumericOperation`.

### Prompt
```
I need a quality rule that derives a composite reporting key for EMIR submissions.
The key must be: <Year>-<CounterpartyLEI>-<AssetClass>
where Year is extracted from TradeDate (format "YYYY-MM-DD").

Please:
1. Design the transform steps using the Controls Platform operation types
2. Generate 4 realistic test cases covering: normal data, a null TradeDate, a null LEI, and a non-standard asset class
3. Test Step 1 — extract the year from TradeDate — by calling POST /transform/execute with your test data
4. Test Step 2 — concatenate Year + CounterpartyLEI + AssetClass — by calling POST /transform/execute with the results from Step 1
5. Show me the full test results in a table
6. Write the two final operation definitions as a JSON array so I can paste them into my control configuration
```

**What this tests:**
- Two sequential `api_call` tool calls to `POST /transform/execute`
- LLM-generated test data and operation payloads
- Multi-step chaining (output of call 1 feeds input of call 2)
- JSON config generation from live test results

**Config Assistant approval flow:**
1. **Approval 1** — `api_call` POST /transform/execute (DateExtract step)
   - Expand "Arguments" to verify the `data` array and `operation` shape before approving
2. **Approval 2** — `api_call` POST /transform/execute (Concatenate step)
   - Check that the `data` array uses the year values returned from Step 1
3. LLM presents results table and JSON config — no further tool calls needed unless you ask it to write to a file

**Example of what the approval card will show for Step 1:**
```json
{
  "endpoint": "/transform/execute",
  "method": "POST",
  "body": {
    "data": [
      {"id": 1, "TradeDate": "2024-01-15", "CounterpartyLEI": "7LTWFZYICNSX8D621K86", "AssetClass": "FX Spot"},
      {"id": 2, "TradeDate": null,          "CounterpartyLEI": "8I5DZWZKVSZI1NUHU748", "AssetClass": "IR Swap"},
      {"id": 3, "TradeDate": "2024-01-16", "CounterpartyLEI": null,                     "AssetClass": "Credit Default"},
      {"id": 4, "TradeDate": "2024-01-17", "CounterpartyLEI": "G5GSEF7VJP5I7OUK5573", "AssetClass": "Commodity"}
    ],
    "operation": {
      "NewFieldName": "TradeYear",
      "Operation": "DateExtract",
      "Inputs": "TradeDate",
      "component": "year",
      "types": "str"
    }
  }
}
```

---

## Example 4 — Conditional Formula + Validator Test + Config Update

**Use in: Config Assistant**
**Also works in: AI Assistant**

### Prompt
```
I want to add a severity-scoring rule to the trade exceptions control.
The rule: if Notional (USD) >= 10,000,000 AND Severity is "High" → score = "Critical", else if Severity is "High" → score = "Elevated", else → score = "Normal".

1. Design this as a Conditional transform operation
2. Create 5 test cases that cover all three outcome branches
3. Call POST /transform/execute to validate the logic against your test data
4. If all 5 results are correct, read D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\control_config.xlsx (Quality Rules sheet) a nd append a new rule row:
   Rule ID: QR-011, Control ID: CTRL-004, Field: severity_score, Rule Type: CONDITIONAL,
   Expression: the formula you built, Error Message: "Severity score miscalculated", Active: Y
5. Write the updated sheet back to the file
```

**What this tests:**
- `api_call` POST /transform/execute — Conditional operation with multi-field logic
- LLM validates its own output against expected values before proceeding
- `read_excel` and `write_excel` conditional on test-pass — the model only updates the config if the live test succeeds
- Full end-to-end: design → test → persist

**Config Assistant approval flow:**
1. **Approval 1** — `api_call` POST /transform/execute — review the Conditional operation body
   - You can add a user note at this step: "also test what happens when Notional is exactly 10000000"
2. **Approval 2** — `read_excel` quality_rules sheet
3. **Approval 3** — `write_excel` updated sheet — expand to verify the new QR-011 row before saving

**Expected Conditional operation shape the LLM should generate:**
```json
{
  "NewFieldName": "severity_score",
  "Operation": "Conditional",
  "Inputs": "Notional (USD),Severity",
  "comparator": ">=",
  "value": "10000000",
  "true_value": "Critical",
  "false_value": "Elevated",
  "types": "str"
}
```
*(The LLM may chain two Conditional steps for the three-branch logic — that's correct behaviour.)*

---

## Bonus: Chained Tools (both assistants)

### Prompt
```
I need a quick summary report:
1. Read D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\trade_exceptions.xlsx (Exception Report sheet)
2. Count how many "Missing LEI" and "Stale LEI" exceptions are currently Open with High severity
3. Read D:\office\rag_app\controldash\test_data\control_config.xlsx (Quality Rules sheet) and find all rules related to LEI validation
4. Write a combined markdown report explaining the current LEI exception exposure and which quality rules are in place to catch them going forward
```

**Config Assistant approval flow:** 4 tool calls total — you approve each one individually and can add context at any step (e.g. on the write step you could say "also include a recommendation section").

        <div class="step-number bg-teal-600 text-white">3</div>

    <!-- Tab: Security -->
    <div id="tab-security" class="tab-content hidden">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold mb-6" style="color: var(--accent)">Security Features</h2>

      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-red-400 mb-2">on-request</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Ask for confirmation on mutating operations</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-green-400 mb-2">auto</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Auto-approve if path is within cwd</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-blue-400 mb-2">auto-edit</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Auto-approve safe, ask for others</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-purple-400 mb-2">never</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Only auto-approve known-safe</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-orange-400 mb-2">on-failure</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Auto-approve, ask on failure</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
          <h4 class="font-semibold text-gray-400 mb-2">yolo</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">No approval checks</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h3 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-red-400">Blocked Dangerous Patterns</h3>
      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-8">
        <div class="code-block">
          <div class="code-header">safety/approval.py:27-54</div>
          <pre><code class="language-python">DANGEROUS_PATTERNS = [
    # File system destruction
    r"rm\s+(-rf?|--recursive)\s+[/~]",
    r"rm\s+-rf?\s+\*",
    r"rmdir\s+[/~]",
    # Disk operations
    r"dd\s+if=",
    r"mkfs", r"fdisk", r"parted",
    # System control
    r"shutdown", r"reboot", r"halt",
    # Permission changes
    r"chmod\s+(-R\s+)?777\s+[/~]",
    # Network exposure
    r"nc\s+-l", r"netcat\s+-l",
    # Code execution from network
    r"curl\s+.*\|\s*(bash|sh)",
    r"wget\s+.*\|\s*(bash|sh)",
    # Fork bomb
    r":\(\)\s*\{\s*:\|:&\s*\}\s*;",
]</code></pre>
        </div>
        <div class="code-block">
          <div class="code-header">safety/approval.py:56-73</div>
          <pre><code class="language-python">SAFE_PATTERNS = [
    # Information commands
    r"^(ls|dir|pwd|cd|echo|cat|head|tail)",
    r"^(find|locate|which|whereis|file|stat)",
    # Git read-only
    r"^git\s+(status|log|diff|show|branch)",
    # Dev tools
    r"^(npm|yarn|pnpm)\s+(list|ls|outdated)",
    r"^pip\s+(list|show|freeze)",
    # Text processing
    r"^(grep|awk|sed|cut|sort|uniq|tr)",
    # System info
    r"^(date|cal|uptime|whoami|id|groups)",
    r"^(env|printenv|set)$",
    r"^(ps|top|htop|pgrep)",
]</code></pre>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h3 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-teal-400">Approval Decision Flow</h3>
      <div class="code-block">
        <div class="code-header">safety/approval.py:129-150</div>
        <pre><code class="language-python">async def check_approval(self, context: ApprovalContext) -> ApprovalDecision:
    if not context.is_mutating:
        return ApprovalDecision.APPROVED

    if context.command:
        decision = self._assess_command_safety(context.command)
        if decision != ApprovalDecision.NEEDS_CONFIRMATION:
            return decision

    for path in context.affected_paths:
        if path.is_relative_to(self.cwd):
            path_decision = ApprovalDecision.APPROVED
        else:
            return ApprovalDecision.NEEDS_CONFIRMATION

    if context.is_dangerous:
        if self.approval_policy == ApprovalPolicy.YOLO:
            return ApprovalDecision.APPROVED
        return ApprovalDecision.NEEDS_CONFIRMATION

    return ApprovalDecision.APPROVED</code></pre>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Tab: Non-Streaming -->
    <div id="tab-non-streaming" class="tab-content hidden">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold mb-6" style="color: var(--accent)">Non-Streaming Response Handling</h2>

      <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-8">
        <div class="section-card rounded-xl p-6">
          <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-3 text-yellow-400">Streaming (_stream_response)</h3>
          <ul class="text-sm text-gray-400 space-y-2">
            <li>• Yields TEXT_DELTA for each content fragment</li>
            <li>• Yields TOOL_CALL_START/DELTA incrementally</li>
            <li>• Yields TOOL_CALL_COMPLETE when tool call finishes</li>
            <li>• Yields MESSAGE_COMPLETE at end with usage</li>
            <li>• Real-time token-by-token display</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
        <div class="section-card rounded-xl p-6">
          <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-3 text-teal-400">Non-Streaming (_non_stream_response)</h3>
          <ul class="text-sm text-gray-400 space-y-2">
            <li>• Yields single TEXT_DELTA with full content</li>
            <li>• Yields TOOL_CALL_COMPLETE for each tool</li>
            <li>• Yields MESSAGE_COMPLETE with usage stats</li>
            <li>• Waits for complete LLM response</li>
            <li>• Both paths converge to same event types</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h3 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-teal-400">Non-Streaming Code Flow</h3>
      <div class="code-block">
        <div class="code-header">client/llm_client.py:219-264</div>
        <pre><code class="language-python">async def _non_stream_response(
    self,
    client: AsyncOpenAI,
    kwargs: dict[str, Any],
) -> AsyncGenerator[StreamEvent, None]:
    response = await self._call_with_retry(client, **kwargs)
    choice = response.choices[0]
    message = choice.message

    # yield text first if present
    if message.content:
        yield StreamEvent(
            type=StreamEventType.TEXT_DELTA,
            text_delta=TextDelta(content=message.content),
        )

    # yield each tool call as a separate TOOL_CALL_COMPLETE event
    if message.tool_calls:
        for tc in message.tool_calls:
            yield StreamEvent(
                type=StreamEventType.TOOL_CALL_COMPLETE,
                tool_call=ToolCall(
                    call_id=tc.id,
                    name=tc.function.name,
                    arguments=parse_tool_call_arguments(tc.function.arguments),
                ),
            )

    usage = None
    if response.usage:
        usage = TokenUsage(
            prompt_tokens=response.usage.prompt_tokens,
            completion_tokens=response.usage.completion_tokens,
            total_tokens=response.usage.total_tokens,
            cached_tokens=(
                response.usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens
                if response.usage.prompt_tokens_details
                else 0
            ),
        )

    yield StreamEvent(
        type=StreamEventType.MESSAGE_COMPLETE,
        finish_reason=choice.finish_reason,
        usage=usage,
    )</code></pre>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Tab: Context Management -->

      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold mb-6" style="color: var(--accent)">Context Management</h2>

      <div class="flex gap-6 mb-8">
        <div class="flex-1 section-card rounded-xl p-6 text-center">

          <div class="text-xs text-gray-500">System prompt + Recent messages<br>within 20k tokens budget</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex-1 section-card rounded-xl p-6 text-center">
          <div class="text-3xl font-bold text-teal-300 mb-2">L1</div>
          <div class="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3">Conditionally Included</div>
          <div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Older messages (+8k tokens)<br>Selected by relevance (min 0.15)</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex-1 section-card rounded-xl p-6 text-center">
          <div class="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2">L2</div>
          <div class="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3">Archived</div>
          <div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Beyond L1 budget<br>Never sent to LLM</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h3 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-teal-400">Tool Output Pruning</h3>
      <div class="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
        <ul class="text-sm text-gray-400 space-y-2">
          <li>• Triggers when context > 60% full</li>
          <li>• Requires at least 2 user messages</li>
          <li>• Protects recent tool outputs (40k tokens)</li>
          <li>• Clears older tool results to preserve context space</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

    function showTab(tabName) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
      document.getElementById('tab-' + tabName).classList.remove('hidden');
      event.target.classList.add('active');
    }
    Prism.highlightAll();
